The trial personnel and the court clerk shall note the service situation on the proof of service and sign it.  
The People's Court may directly serve litigation documents on a party outside the party's domicile. If the party refuses to sign the proof of service, recording the service process by photographing, video recording, or other means shall be deemed service. The trial personnel and the court clerk shall note the service situation on the proof of service and sign it.

<a id="art-132"></a>第一百三十二条　受送达人有诉讼代理人的，人民法院既可以向受送达人送达，也可以向其诉讼代理人送达。受送达人指定诉讼代理人为代收人的，向诉讼代理人送达时，适用留置送达。

**Article 132** Where the person to be served has a litigation agent, the People's Court may serve the documents on the person to be served or on the litigation agent. Where the person to be served designates the litigation agent as the recipient on his or her behalf, service by leaving the documents at the place of service shall apply when serving on the litigation agent.

<a id="art-133"></a>第一百三十三条　调解书应当直接送达当事人本人，不适用留置送达。当事人本人因故不能签收的，可由其指定的代收人签收。

**Article 133** A mediation document shall be served directly on the party himself or herself and shall not be subject to service by leaving the documents at the place of service. If the party cannot sign for receipt due to reasons, the recipient designated by the party may sign for receipt.

<a id="art-134"></a>第一百三十四条　依照[民事诉讼法第九十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-91)规定，委托其他人民法院代为送达的，委托法院应当出具委托函，并附需要送达的诉讼文书和送达回证，以受送达人在送达回证上签收的日期为送达日期。  
委托送达的，受委托人民法院应当自收到委托函及相关诉讼文书之日起十日内代为送达。

**Article 134** Where, in accordance with Article 91 of the Civil Procedure Law, service is entrusted to another People's Court, the entrusting court shall issue a letter of entrustment, together with the litigation documents to be served and the proof of service. The date on which the person to be served signs for receipt on the proof of service shall be the date of service.  
For entrusted service, the entrusted People's Court shall complete the service on behalf of the entrusting court within ten days from the date of receipt of the letter of entrustment and the relevant litigation documents.

<a id="art-135"></a>第一百三十五条　电子送达可以采用传真、电子邮件、移动通信等即时收悉的特定系统作为送达媒介。  
[民事诉讼法第九十条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-90)第二款规定的到达受送达人特定系统的日期，为人民法院对应系统显示发送成功的日期，但受送达人证明到达其特定系统的日期与人民法院对应系统显示发送成功的日期不一致的，以受送达人证明到达其特定系统的日期为准。

**Article 135** Electronic service may adopt specific systems for immediate receipt, such as facsimile, e-mail, mobile communication, or other similar systems, as the medium for service.  
The date on which a document reaches the specific system of the person to be served, as provided for in paragraph 2 of Article 90 of the Civil Procedure Law, shall be the date on which the corresponding system of the People's Court displays successful transmission. However, if the person to be served proves that the date on which the document reached his or her specific system is inconsistent with the date on which the corresponding system of the People's Court displays successful transmission, the date proved by the person to be served as the date of arrival at his or her specific system shall prevail.

<a id="art-136"></a>第一百三十六条　受送达人同意采用电子方式送达的，应当在送达地址确认书中予以确认。

**Article 136** If the person to be served agrees to service by electronic means, the agreement shall be confirmed in the service address confirmation statement.

<a id="art-137"></a>第一百三十七条　当事人在提起上诉、申请再审、申请执行时未书面变更送达地址的，其在第一审程序中确认的送达地址可以作为第二审程序、审判监督程序、执行程序的送达地址。

**Article 137** If a party does not change the service address in writing when filing an appeal, applying for retrial, or applying for enforcement, the service address confirmed in the first-instance procedure may be used as the service address for the second-instance procedure, the trial supervision procedure, and the enforcement procedure.

<a id="art-138"></a>第一百三十八条　公告送达可以在法院的公告栏和受送达人住所地张贴公告，也可以在报纸、信息网络等媒体上刊登公告，发出公告日期以最后张贴或者刊登的日期为准。对公告送达方式有特殊要求的，应当按要求的方式进行。公告期满，即视为送达。  
人民法院在受送达人住所地张贴公告的，应当采取拍照、录像等方式记录张贴过程。

**Article 138** Service by public announcement may be made by posting a notice on the court's notice board and at the domicile of the person to be served, or by publishing a notice in newspapers, information networks, or other media. The date of issuing the public announcement shall be the date of the last posting or publication. If there are special requirements for the method of service by public announcement, service shall be made in the required manner. Upon expiration of the announcement period, service shall be deemed completed.  
Where the People's Court posts a notice at the domicile of the person to be served, it shall record the posting process by photographing, video recording, or other means.

<a id="art-139"></a>第一百三十九条　公告送达应当说明公告送达的原因；公告送达起诉状或者上诉状副本的，应当说明起诉或者上诉要点，受送达人答辩期限及逾期不答辩的法律后果；公告送达传票，应当说明出庭的时间和地点及逾期不出庭的法律后果；公告送达判决书、裁定书的，应当说明裁判主要内容，当事人有权上诉的，还应当说明上诉权利、上诉期限和上诉的人民法院。

**Article 139** Service by public announcement shall state the reasons for service by public announcement; for service by public announcement of a copy of the statement of complaint or a copy of the statement of appeal, it shall state the main points of the prosecution or appeal, the time limit for the person to be served to answer, and the legal consequences of failing to answer within the time limit; for service by public announcement of a summons, it shall state the time and place of appearance in court and the legal consequences of failing to appear in court within the time limit; for service by public announcement of a written judgment or written ruling, it shall state the main contents of the adjudication, and if the party has the right to appeal, it shall also state the right to appeal, the time limit for appeal, and the People's Court to which the appeal shall be made.

<a id="art-140"></a>第一百四十条　适用简易程序的案件，不适用公告送达。

**Article 140** Service by public announcement shall not apply to cases subject to the summary procedure.

<a id="art-141"></a>第一百四十一条　人民法院在定期宣判时，当事人拒不签收判决书、裁定书的，应视为送达，并在宣判笔录中记明。

**Article 141** If a party refuses to sign for receipt of a written judgment or written ruling when the People's Court pronounces a judgment at a scheduled session, service shall be deemed completed, and the matter shall be recorded in the record of pronouncement.

### 六、调解

<a id="art-142"></a>第一百四十二条　人民法院受理案件后，经审查，认为法律关系明确、事实清楚，在征得当事人双方同意后，可以径行调解。

**Article 142** After accepting a case and upon examination, if the People's Court considers that the legal relationship is clear and the facts are evident, it may conduct mediation directly after obtaining the consent of both parties.

<a id="art-143"></a>第一百四十三条　适用特别程序、督促程序、公示催告程序的案件，婚姻等身份关系确认案件以及其他根据案件性质不能进行调解的案件，不得调解。

**Article 143** Cases subject to the special procedure, the supervision procedure, or the procedure for public summons and exhortation, cases involving confirmation of marital or other status relationships, and other cases that cannot be mediated by their nature shall not be mediated.

<a id="art-144"></a>第一百四十四条　人民法院审理民事案件，发现当事人之间恶意串通，企图通过和解、调解方式侵害他人合法权益的，应当依照[民事诉讼法第一百一十五条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-115)的规定处理。

**Article 144** When trying civil cases, if the People's Court discovers that the parties maliciously collude and attempt to infringe upon the lawful rights and interests of others through settlement or mediation, it shall handle the matter in accordance with Article 115 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-145"></a>第一百四十五条　人民法院审理民事案件，应当根据自愿、合法的原则进行调解。当事人一方或者双方坚持不愿调解的，应当及时裁判。  
人民法院审理离婚案件，应当进行调解，但不应久调不决。

**Article 145** The People's Court shall conduct mediation in civil cases on the principles of voluntariness and lawfulness. If one or both parties insist on not mediating, the court shall render a judgment without delay.  
The People's Court shall conduct mediation in divorce cases, but shall not delay a decision by prolonged mediation.

<a id="art-146"></a>第一百四十六条　人民法院审理民事案件，调解过程不公开，但当事人同意公开的除外。  
调解协议内容不公开，但为保护国家利益、社会公共利益、他人合法权益，人民法院认为确有必要公开的除外。  
主持调解以及参与调解的人员，对调解过程以及调解过程中获悉的国家秘密、商业秘密、个人隐私和其他不宜公开的信息，应当保守秘密，但为保护国家利益、社会公共利益、他人合法权益的除外。

**Article 146** In civil cases tried by the People's Court, the mediation process shall not be public, unless the parties agree to make it public.  
The contents of a mediation agreement shall not be public, unless the People's Court considers it truly necessary to make them public for the protection of national interests, social public interests, or the lawful rights and interests of others.  
The persons presiding over or participating in mediation shall keep confidential the mediation process and any state secrets, trade secrets, personal privacy, and other information not suitable for disclosure obtained during the mediation process, except where disclosure is necessary for the protection of national interests, social public interests, or the lawful rights and interests of others.

<a id="art-147"></a>第一百四十七条　人民法院调解案件时，当事人不能出庭的，经其特别授权，可由其委托代理人参加调解，达成的调解协议，可由委托代理人签名。  
离婚案件当事人确因特殊情况无法出庭参加调解的，除本人不能表达意志的以外，应当出具书面意见。

**Article 147** When the People's Court mediates a case, if a party cannot appear in court, the party's authorized agent may participate in the mediation upon special authorization, and the mediation agreement reached may be signed by the authorized agent.  
If a party in a divorce case truly cannot appear in court to participate in mediation due to special circumstances, the party shall issue a written opinion, unless the party is unable to express his or her will.

<a id="art-148"></a>第一百四十八条　当事人自行和解或者调解达成协议后，请求人民法院按照和解协议或者调解协议的内容制作判决书的，人民法院不予准许。  
无民事行为能力人的离婚案件，由其法定代理人进行诉讼。法定代理人与对方达成协议要求发给判决书的，可根据协议内容制作判决书。

**Article 148** After the parties reach an agreement through settlement or mediation on their own, the People's Court shall not grant a request for the court to make a written judgment in accordance with the contents of the settlement agreement or mediation agreement.  
For a divorce case of a person without capacity for civil conduct, the statutory agent shall conduct the litigation. If the statutory agent reaches an agreement with the other party and requests that a written judgment be issued, a written judgment may be made in accordance with the contents of the agreement.

<a id="art-149"></a>第一百四十九条　调解书需经当事人签收后才发生法律效力的，应当以最后收到调解书的当事人签收的日期为调解书生效日期。

**Article 149** Where a mediation document takes legal effect only after being signed for by the parties, the date on which the party who last receives the mediation document signs for receipt shall be the effective date of the mediation document.

<a id="art-150"></a>第一百五十条　人民法院调解民事案件，需由无独立请求权的第三人承担责任的，应当经其同意。该第三人在调解书送达前反悔的，人民法院应当及时裁判。

**Article 150** Where the mediation of a civil case by the People's Court requires a third party with no independent claim to bear liability, the consent of the third party shall be obtained. If the third party retracts before the mediation document is served, the People's Court shall render a judgment without delay.

<a id="art-151"></a>第一百五十一条　根据[民事诉讼法第一百零一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-101)第一款第四项规定，当事人各方同意在调解协议上签名或者盖章后即发生法律效力的，经人民法院审查确认后，应当记入笔录或者将调解协议附卷，并由当事人、审判人员、书记员签名或者盖章后即具有法律效力。  
前款规定情形，当事人请求制作调解书的，人民法院审查确认后可以制作调解书送交当事人。当事人拒收调解书的，不影响调解协议的效力。

**Article 151** In accordance with item (4) of paragraph 1 of Article 101 of the Civil Procedure Law, where all parties agree that the mediation agreement shall take legal effect upon signature or sealing, after review and confirmation by the People's Court, it shall be recorded in the transcript or attached to the case file, and shall have legal effect after being signed or sealed by the parties, the trial personnel, and the court clerk.  
In the circumstance provided in the preceding paragraph, if a party requests the making of a mediation document, the People's Court may, after review and confirmation, make a mediation document and deliver it to the parties. A party's refusal to accept the mediation document shall not affect the validity of the mediation agreement.

### 七、保全和先予执行

<a id="art-152"></a>第一百五十二条　人民法院依照[民事诉讼法第一百零三条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-103)、第一百零四条规定，在采取诉前保全、诉讼保全措施时，责令利害关系人或者当事人提供担保的，应当书面通知。  
利害关系人申请诉前保全的，应当提供担保。申请诉前财产保全的，应当提供相当于请求保全数额的担保；情况特殊的，人民法院可以酌情处理。申请诉前行为保全的，担保的数额由人民法院根据案件的具体情况决定。  
在诉讼中，人民法院依申请或者依职权采取保全措施的，应当根据案件的具体情况，决定当事人是否应当提供担保以及担保的数额。

**Article 152** Where the People's Court, in accordance with Articles 103 and 104 of the Civil Procedure Law, orders an interested person or a party to provide security when taking pre-litigation preservation or litigation preservation measures, it shall notify in writing.  
An interested person applying for pre-litigation preservation shall provide security. For an application for pre-litigation property preservation, security equivalent to the amount requested for preservation shall be provided; under special circumstances, the People's Court may handle the matter at its discretion. For an application for pre-litigation conduct preservation, the amount of security shall be determined by the People's Court based on the specific circumstances of the case.  
In litigation, where the People's Court takes preservation measures upon application or ex officio, it shall determine, based on the specific circumstances of the case, whether the party shall provide security and the amount of security.

<a id="art-153"></a>第一百五十三条　人民法院对季节性商品、鲜活、易腐烂变质以及其他不宜长期保存的物品采取保全措施时，可以责令当事人及时处理，由人民法院保存价款；必要时，人民法院可予以变卖，保存价款。

**Article 153** When the People's Court takes preservation measures on seasonal goods, fresh and live goods, perishable goods, and other articles not suitable for long-term storage, it may order the party to dispose of them promptly and have the People's Court preserve the proceeds; if necessary, the People's Court may sell them and preserve the proceeds.

<a id="art-154"></a>第一百五十四条　人民法院在财产保全中采取查封、扣押、冻结财产措施时，应当妥善保管被查封、扣押、冻结的财产。不宜由人民法院保管的，人民法院可以指定被保全人负责保管；不宜由被保全人保管的，可以委托他人或者申请保全人保管。  
查封、扣押、冻结担保物权人占有的担保财产，一般由担保物权人保管；由人民法院保管的，质权、留置权不因采取保全措施而消灭。

**Article 154** When the People's Court takes measures to seal up, seize, or freeze property in property preservation, it shall properly keep the sealed-up, seized, or frozen property. Where it is not appropriate for the People's Court to keep the property, the People's Court may designate the person whose property is preserved to be responsible for keeping it; where it is not appropriate for the person whose property is preserved to keep the property, the People's Court may entrust another person or the applicant for preservation to keep it.  
Where the secured property in the possession of a security interest holder is sealed up, seized, or frozen, it shall generally be kept by the security interest holder; where it is kept by the People's Court, the pledge and lien shall not be extinguished by the taking of preservation measures.

<a id="art-155"></a>第一百五十五条　由人民法院指定被保全人保管的财产，如果继续使用对该财产的价值无重大影响，可以允许被保全人继续使用；由人民法院保管或者委托他人、申请保全人保管的财产，人民法院和其他保管人不得使用。

**Article 155** Where the People's Court designates the person whose property is preserved to keep the property, if continued use would not have a significant impact on the value of the property, the person whose property is preserved may be allowed to continue using it; where the People's Court keeps the property or entrusts it to another person or the applicant for preservation, the People's Court and other custodians shall not use it.

<a id="art-156"></a>第一百五十六条　人民法院采取财产保全的方法和措施，依照执行程序相关规定办理。

**Article 156** The methods and measures for property preservation taken by the People's Court shall be handled in accordance with the relevant provisions on enforcement procedures.

<a id="art-157"></a>第一百五十七条　人民法院对抵押物、质押物、留置物可以采取财产保全措施，但不影响抵押权人、质权人、留置权人的优先受偿权。

**Article 157** The People's Court may take property preservation measures on mortgaged property, pledged property, or retained property, but this shall not affect the right of the mortgagee, pledgee, or lienholder to be paid in priority.

<a id="art-158"></a>第一百五十八条　人民法院对债务人到期应得的收益，可以采取财产保全措施，限制其支取，通知有关单位协助执行。

**Article 158** The People's Court may take property preservation measures on a debtor's due and receivable income, restrict the withdrawal thereof, and notify the relevant unit to assist in enforcement.

<a id="art-159"></a>第一百五十九条　债务人的财产不能满足保全请求，但对他人有到期债权的，人民法院可以依债权人的申请裁定该他人不得对本案债务人清偿。该他人要求偿付的，由人民法院提存财物或者价款。

**Article 159** If the debtor's property cannot satisfy the preservation claim, but the debtor has a due claim against another person, the People's Court may, upon the creditor's application, rule that such other person shall not pay off the debtor in the case. If the other person requests payment, the People's Court shall deposit the property or the payment with the court.

<a id="art-160"></a>第一百六十条　当事人向采取诉前保全措施以外的其他有管辖权的人民法院起诉的，采取诉前保全措施的人民法院应当将保全手续移送受理案件的人民法院。诉前保全的裁定视为受移送人民法院作出的裁定。

**Article 160** Where a party brings an action in another People's Court that has jurisdiction rather than the court that took the pre-litigation preservation measures, the People's Court that took the pre-litigation preservation measures shall transfer the preservation procedures to the People's Court accepting the case. The ruling on pre-litigation preservation shall be deemed to be a ruling made by the People's Court to which the case is transferred.

<a id="art-161"></a>第一百六十一条　对当事人不服一审判决提起上诉的案件，在第二审人民法院接到报送的案件之前，当事人有转移、隐匿、出卖或者毁损财产等行为，必须采取保全措施的，由第一审人民法院依当事人申请或者依职权采取。第一审人民法院的保全裁定，应当及时报送第二审人民法院。

**Article 161** For a case in which a party appeals against a first-instance judgment, before the second-instance People's Court receives the transferred case, if the party commits acts such as transferring, concealing, selling, or destroying property and preservation measures must be taken, the first-instance People's Court shall take them upon the party's application or ex officio. The preservation ruling of the first-instance People's Court shall be promptly transmitted to the second-instance People's Court.

<a id="art-162"></a>第一百六十二条　第二审人民法院裁定对第一审人民法院采取的保全措施予以续保或者采取新的保全措施的，可以自行实施，也可以委托第一审人民法院实施。  
再审人民法院裁定对原保全措施予以续保或者采取新的保全措施的，可以自行实施，也可以委托原审人民法院或者执行法院实施。

**Article 162** Where the second-instance People's Court rules to extend the preservation measures taken by the first-instance People's Court or to take new preservation measures, it may implement them itself or entrust the first-instance People's Court to implement them.  
Where the People's Court conducting retrial rules to extend the original preservation measures or to take new preservation measures, it may implement them itself or entrust the court that originally tried the case or the enforcement court to implement them.

<a id="art-163"></a>第一百六十三条　法律文书生效后，进入执行程序前，债权人因对方当事人转移财产等紧急情况，不申请保全将可能导致生效法律文书不能执行或者难以执行的，可以向执行法院申请采取保全措施。债权人在法律文书指定的履行期间届满后五日内不申请执行的，人民法院应当解除保全。

**Article 163** After a legal instrument takes effect and before the enforcement procedure commences, if, due to emergency circumstances such as the other party's transfer of property, failure to apply for preservation would likely cause the effective legal instrument to be unenforceable or difficult to enforce, the creditor may apply to the enforcement court for preservation measures. If the creditor does not apply for enforcement within five days after the expiration of the performance period specified in the legal instrument, the People's Court shall dissolve the preservation.

<a id="art-164"></a>第一百六十四条　对申请保全人或者他人提供的担保财产，人民法院应当依法办理查封、扣押、冻结等手续。

**Article 164** For the security property provided by the applicant for preservation or another person, the People's Court shall, according to law, complete the procedures for sealing up, seizure, freezing, and other matters.

<a id="art-165"></a>第一百六十五条　人民法院裁定采取保全措施后，除作出保全裁定的人民法院自行解除或者其上级人民法院决定解除外，在保全期限内，任何单位不得解除保全措施。

**Article 165** After the People's Court rules to take preservation measures, no unit shall dissolve the preservation measures within the preservation period, except where the People's Court that made the preservation ruling dissolves them on its own or its higher-level People's Court decides to dissolve them.

<a id="art-166"></a>第一百六十六条　裁定采取保全措施后，有下列情形之一的，人民法院应当作出解除保全裁定：  
(一)保全错误的；  
(二)申请人撤回保全申请的；  
(三)申请人的起诉或者诉讼请求被生效裁判驳回的；  
(四)人民法院认为应当解除保全的其他情形。  
解除以登记方式实施的保全措施的，应当向登记机关发出协助执行通知书。

**Article 166** After ruling to take preservation measures, under any of the following circumstances, the People's Court shall make a ruling to dissolve the preservation:  
(1) the preservation is erroneous;  
(2) the applicant withdraws the preservation application;  
(3) the applicant's prosecution or litigation claims are rejected by an effective judgment or ruling;  
(4) there are other circumstances under which the People's Court considers the preservation shall be dissolved.  
To dissolve preservation measures implemented by way of registration, a notice to assist in enforcement shall be issued to the registration authority.

<a id="art-167"></a>第一百六十七条　财产保全的被保全人提供其他等值担保财产且有利于执行的，人民法院可以裁定变更保全标的物为被保全人提供的担保财产。

**Article 167** If the person whose property is preserved in property preservation provides other security property of equal value and it is conducive to enforcement, the People's Court may rule to change the subject matter of preservation to the security property provided by the person whose property is preserved.

<a id="art-168"></a>第一百六十八条　保全裁定未经人民法院依法撤销或者解除，进入执行程序后，自动转为执行中的查封、扣押、冻结措施，期限连续计算，执行法院无需重新制作裁定书，但查封、扣押、冻结期限届满的除外。

**Article 168** If a preservation ruling has not been revoked or dissolved by the People's Court according to law, after entering the enforcement procedure, it shall automatically be converted into the measures of sealing up, seizure, and freezing during enforcement, and the time limits shall be calculated continuously. The enforcement court need not make a new written ruling, except where the time limit for sealing up, seizure, or freezing has expired.

<a id="art-169"></a>第一百六十九条　民事诉讼法规定的先予执行，人民法院应当在受理案件后终审判决作出前采取。先予执行应当限于当事人诉讼请求的范围，并以当事人的生活、生产经营的急需为限。

**Article 169** The advance execution provided for in the Civil Procedure Law shall be taken by the People's Court after accepting a case and before a final-instance judgment is rendered. Advance execution shall be limited to the scope of the party's claims and to the urgent needs of the party's livelihood and production and business operations.

<a id="art-170"></a>第一百七十条　[民事诉讼法第一百零九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-109)第三项规定的情况紧急，包括：  
（一）需要立即停止侵害、排除妨碍的；  
（二）需要立即制止某项行为的；  
（三）追索恢复生产、经营急需的保险理赔费的；  
（四）需要立即返还社会保险金、社会救助资金的；  
（五）不立即返还款项，将严重影响权利人生活和生产经营的。

**Article 170** The urgent circumstances provided for in item (3) of Article 109 of the Civil Procedure Law include:  
(1) where it is necessary to immediately stop the infringement or remove the obstruction;  
(2) where it is necessary to immediately stop a certain act;  
(3) where claiming insurance compensation urgently needed for the resumption of production or business operations;  
(4) where it is necessary to immediately return social insurance benefits or social assistance funds;  
(5) where failure to immediately return the funds would seriously affect the right holder's livelihood, production, or business operations.

<a id="art-171"></a>第一百七十一条　当事人对保全或者先予执行裁定不服的，可以自收到裁定书之日起五日内向作出裁定的人民法院申请复议。人民法院应当在收到复议申请后十日内审查。裁定正确的，驳回当事人的申请；裁定不当的，变更或者撤销原裁定。

**Article 171** If a party is dissatisfied with a ruling on preservation or advance execution, the party may, within five days from the date of receipt of the written ruling, apply to the People's Court that made the ruling for reconsideration. The People's Court shall review the application within ten days after receipt. If the ruling is correct, the party's application shall be dismissed; if the ruling is improper, the original ruling shall be changed or revoked.

<a id="art-172"></a>第一百七十二条　利害关系人对保全或者先予执行的裁定不服申请复议的，由作出裁定的人民法院依照[民事诉讼法第一百一十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-111)规定处理。

**Article 172** If an interested person applies for reconsideration because of dissatisfaction with a ruling on preservation or advance execution, the People's Court that made the ruling shall handle the matter in accordance with Article 111 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-173"></a>第一百七十三条　人民法院先予执行后，根据发生法律效力的判决，申请人应当返还因先予执行所取得的利益的，适用[民事诉讼法第二百四十条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-240)的规定。

**Article 173** Where, after the People's Court carries out advance execution and according to the judgment that has taken legal effect, the applicant shall return the benefits obtained from the advance execution, the provisions of Article 240 of the Civil Procedure Law shall apply.

### 八、对妨害民事诉讼的强制措施

<a id="art-174"></a>第一百七十四条　[民事诉讼法第一百一十二条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-112)规定的必须到庭的被告，是指负有赡养、抚育、扶养义务和不到庭就无法查清案情的被告。  
人民法院对必须到庭才能查清案件基本事实的原告，经两次传票传唤，无正当理由拒不到庭的，可以拘传。

**Article 174** . A defendant who is required to appear in court as provided in Article 112 of the Civil Procedure Law is a defendant who bears the obligations of support, upbringing, and maintenance, and whose case cannot be clearly ascertained if such defendant fails to appear in court.  
Where a plaintiff whose presence is necessary to ascertain the basic facts of the case, after being summoned twice by summons, refuses to appear in court without justifiable reasons, the People's Court may compel the plaintiff to appear.

<a id="art-175"></a>第一百七十五条　拘传必须用拘传票，并直接送达被拘传人；在拘传前，应当向被拘传人说明拒不到庭的后果，经批评教育仍拒不到庭的，可以拘传其到庭。

**Article 175** . Compulsory appearance shall be made by a compulsory appearance warrant and served directly on the person to be compelled to appear. Before compelling appearance, the consequences of refusing to appear in court shall be explained to the person; if, after criticism and education, the person still refuses to appear, the person may be compelled to appear in court.

<a id="art-176"></a>第一百七十六条　诉讼参与人或者其他人有下列行为之一的，人民法院可以适用[民事诉讼法第一百一十三条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-113)规定处理：  
(一)未经准许进行录音、录像、摄影的；  
(二)未经准许以移动通信等方式现场传播审判活动的；  
(三)其他扰乱法庭秩序，妨害审判活动进行的。  
有前款规定情形的，人民法院可以暂扣诉讼参与人或者其他人进行录音、录像、摄影、传播审判活动的器材，并责令其删除有关内容；拒不删除的，人民法院可以采取必要手段强制删除。

**Article 176** . Where any litigation participant or other person commits any of the following acts, the People's Court may handle the matter in accordance with the provisions of Article 113 of the Civil Procedure Law:  
(1) making audio recordings, video recordings, or taking photographs without permission;  
(2) broadcasting trial activities on site by means of mobile communications or otherwise without permission;  
(3) other acts disturbing court order and obstructing trial proceedings.  
In any of the circumstances set forth in the preceding paragraph, the People's Court may temporarily seize the equipment used by the litigation participant or other person for audio recording, video recording, photographing, or broadcasting trial activities, and order the deletion of the relevant content; if the person refuses to delete, the People's Court may take necessary measures to force deletion.

<a id="art-177"></a>第一百七十七条　训诫、责令退出法庭由合议庭或者独任审判员决定。训诫的内容、被责令退出法庭者的违法事实应当记入庭审笔录。

**Article 177** . An admonition or an order to leave the courtroom shall be decided by the collegial panel or the sole judge. The content of the admonition and the unlawful facts of the person ordered to leave the courtroom shall be recorded in the trial transcript.

<a id="art-178"></a>第一百七十八条　人民法院依照[民事诉讼法第一百一十三条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-113)至第一百一十七条的规定采取拘留措施的，应经院长批准，作出拘留决定书，由司法警察将被拘留人送交当地公安机关看管。

**Article 178** . Where the People's Court takes detention measures in accordance with the provisions of Articles 113 to 117 of the Civil Procedure Law, it shall obtain the approval of the president of the court, issue a written detention decision, and have the judicial police deliver the detained person to the local public security organ for custody.

<a id="art-179"></a>第一百七十九条　被拘留人不在本辖区的，作出拘留决定的人民法院应当派员到被拘留人所在地的人民法院，请该院协助执行，受委托的人民法院应当及时派员协助执行。被拘留人申请复议或者在拘留期间承认并改正错误，需要提前解除拘留的，受委托人民法院应当向委托人民法院转达或者提出建议，由委托人民法院审查决定。

**Article 179** . If the detained person is not within the territorial jurisdiction of the court, the People's Court that made the detention decision shall send personnel to the People's Court where the detained person is located to request that court to assist in the execution. The entrusted People's Court shall promptly send personnel to assist in the execution. If the detained person applies for reconsideration, or acknowledges and corrects the mistake during detention and the detention needs to be lifted ahead of time, the entrusted People's Court shall convey the request to the entrusting People's Court or make a recommendation, and the entrusting People's Court shall review and decide.

<a id="art-180"></a>第一百八十条　人民法院对被拘留人采取拘留措施后，应当在二十四小时内通知其家属；确实无法按时通知或者通知不到的，应当记录在案。

**Article 180** . After the People's Court imposes detention on a detained person, it shall notify the detained person's family members within twenty-four hours; if it is truly impossible to notify them on time or they cannot be reached, it shall record the matter in the files.

<a id="art-181"></a>第一百八十一条　因哄闹、冲击法庭，用暴力、威胁等方法抗拒执行公务等紧急情况，必须立即采取拘留措施的，可在拘留后，立即报告院长补办批准手续。院长认为拘留不当的，应当解除拘留。

**Article 181** . In emergency circumstances such as creating a disturbance at or storming the courtroom, or using violence, threats, or other methods to resist the performance of official duties, if detention measures must be taken immediately, the court may, after the detention, immediately report to the president to complete the approval formalities after the fact. If the president considers the detention improper, the detention shall be lifted.

<a id="art-182"></a>第一百八十二条　被拘留人在拘留期间认错悔改的，可以责令其具结悔过，提前解除拘留。提前解除拘留，应报经院长批准，并作出提前解除拘留决定书，交负责看管的公安机关执行。

**Article 182** . If the detained person admits the mistake and repents during detention, the People's Court may order the person to sign a statement of repentance and release the person from detention ahead of time. Early release from detention shall be subject to the approval of the president of the court, and a written decision on early release shall be issued and handed over to the public security organ responsible for custody for enforcement.

<a id="art-183"></a>第一百八十三条　[民事诉讼法第一百一十三条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-113)至第一百一十六条规定的罚款、拘留可以单独适用，也可以合并适用。

**Article 183** . The fine and detention prescribed in Articles 113 to 116 of the Civil Procedure Law may be applied separately or concurrently.

<a id="art-184"></a>第一百八十四条　对同一妨害民事诉讼行为的罚款、拘留不得连续适用。发生新的妨害民事诉讼行为的，人民法院可以重新予以罚款、拘留。

**Article 184** . A fine or detention shall not be imposed consecutively for the same act obstructing civil litigation. If a new act obstructing civil litigation occurs, the People's Court may impose a fine or detention anew.

<a id="art-185"></a>第一百八十五条　被罚款、拘留的人不服罚款、拘留决定申请复议的，应当自收到决定书之日起三日内提出。上级人民法院应当在收到复议申请后五日内作出决定，并将复议结果通知下级人民法院和当事人。

**Article 185** . If a person fined or detained refuses to accept the decision on fine or detention and applies for reconsideration, the application shall be filed within three days from the date of receipt of the written decision. The higher People's Court shall make a decision within five days after receiving the application for reconsideration and notify the lower People's Court and the parties of the reconsideration result.

<a id="art-186"></a>第一百八十六条　上级人民法院复议时认为强制措施不当的，应当制作决定书，撤销或者变更下级人民法院作出的拘留、罚款决定。情况紧急的，可以在口头通知后三日内发出决定书。

**Article 186** . If, upon reconsideration, the higher People's Court considers the compulsory measures improper, it shall make a written decision to revoke or modify the detention or fine decision made by the lower People's Court. In urgent circumstances, the written decision may be issued within three days after oral notification.

<a id="art-187"></a>第一百八十七条　[民事诉讼法第一百一十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-114)第一款第五项规定的以暴力、威胁或者其他方法阻碍司法工作人员执行职务的行为，包括：  
(一)在人民法院哄闹、滞留，不听从司法工作人员劝阻的；  
(二)故意毁损、抢夺人民法院法律文书、查封标志的；  
(三)哄闹、冲击执行公务现场，围困、扣押执行或者协助执行公务人员的；  
(四)毁损、抢夺、扣留案件材料、执行公务车辆、其他执行公务器械、执行公务人员服装和执行公务证件的；  
(五)以暴力、威胁或者其他方法阻碍司法工作人员查询、查封、扣押、冻结、划拨、拍卖、变卖财产的；  
(六)以暴力、威胁或者其他方法阻碍司法工作人员执行职务的其他行为。

**Article 187** . The acts of using violence, threats, or other methods to obstruct judicial personnel in performing official duties as provided in item 5 of paragraph 1 of Article 114 of the Civil Procedure Law include:  
(1) creating a disturbance or lingering in the People's Court and refusing to heed the dissuasion of judicial personnel;  
(2) intentionally destroying or seizing the legal instruments or sealing marks of the People's Court;  
(3) creating a disturbance at or storming the scene of official enforcement, or besieging or detaining persons executing or assisting in executing official duties;  
(4) destroying, seizing, or detaining case materials, official enforcement vehicles, other enforcement instruments, uniforms of enforcement personnel, or enforcement credentials;  
(5) using violence, threats, or other methods to obstruct judicial personnel from inquiring, sealing, seizing, freezing, transferring, auctioning, or selling property;  
(6) other acts that use violence, threats, or other methods to obstruct judicial personnel in performing official duties.

<a id="art-188"></a>第一百八十八条　[民事诉讼法第一百一十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-114)第一款第六项规定的拒不履行人民法院已经发生法律效力的判决、裁定的行为，包括：  
(一)在法律文书发生法律效力后隐藏、转移、变卖、毁损财产或者无偿转让财产、以明显不合理的价格交易财产、放弃到期债权、无偿为他人提供担保等，致使人民法院无法执行的；  
(二)隐藏、转移、毁损或者未经人民法院允许处分已向人民法院提供担保的财产的；  
(三)违反人民法院限制高消费令进行消费的；  
(四)有履行能力而拒不按照人民法院执行通知履行生效法律文书确定的义务的；  
(五)有义务协助执行的个人接到人民法院协助执行通知书后，拒不协助执行的。

**Article 188** . The acts of refusing to perform a judgment or ruling of the People's Court that has taken legal effect as provided in item 6 of paragraph 1 of Article 114 of the Civil Procedure Law include:  
(1) after the legal instrument takes legal effect, concealing, transferring, selling off, or destroying property, or gratuitously transferring property, trading property at an obviously unreasonable price, abandoning due claims, gratuitously providing security for others, etc., thereby making it impossible for the People's Court to enforce;  
(2) concealing, transferring, destroying, or disposing of, without permission of the People's Court, property that has been provided as security to the People's Court;  
(3) consuming in violation of the People's Court's order restricting high consumption;  
(4) having the ability to perform but refusing to perform the obligations determined by the effective legal instrument in accordance with the enforcement notice of the People's Court;  
(5) where an individual obligated to assist in enforcement, after receiving the notice of assistance in enforcement from the People's Court, refuses to assist in enforcement.

<a id="art-189"></a>第一百八十九条　诉讼参与人或者其他人有下列行为之一的，人民法院可以适用[民事诉讼法第一百一十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-114)的规定处理：  
(一)冒充他人提起诉讼或者参加诉讼的；  
(二)证人签署保证书后作虚假证言，妨碍人民法院审理案件的；  
(三)伪造、隐藏、毁灭或者拒绝交出有关被执行人履行能力的重要证据，妨碍人民法院查明被执行人财产状况的；  
(四)擅自解冻已被人民法院冻结的财产的；  
(五)接到人民法院协助执行通知书后，给当事人通风报信，协助其转移、隐匿财产的。

**Article 189** . Where any litigation participant or other person commits any of the following acts, the People's Court may handle the matter in accordance with the provisions of Article 114 of the Civil Procedure Law:  
(1) impersonating another person to bring an action or participate in litigation;  
(2) a witness making false testimony after signing an undertaking, thereby obstructing the People's Court in hearing the case;  
(3) forging, concealing, destroying, or refusing to surrender important evidence concerning the property execution ability of the person subject to execution, thereby obstructing the People's Court from ascertaining the property status of the person subject to execution;  
(4) unfreezing, without authorization, property that has been frozen by the People's Court;  
(5) after receiving the notice of assistance in enforcement from the People's Court, tipping off the party and assisting the party in transferring or concealing property.

<a id="art-190"></a>第一百九十条　[民事诉讼法第一百一十五条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-115)规定的他人合法权益，包括案外人的合法权益、国家利益、社会公共利益。  
第三人根据[民事诉讼法第五十九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-59)第三款规定提起撤销之诉，经审查，原案当事人之间恶意串通进行虚假诉讼的，适用[民事诉讼法第一百一十五条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-115)规定处理。

**Article 190** . The lawful rights and interests of other persons as provided in Article 115 of the Civil Procedure Law include the lawful rights and interests of persons not involved in the case, the national interest, and the social public interest.  
Where a third party files an action for revocation in accordance with paragraph 3 of Article 59 of the Civil Procedure Law, and upon examination it is found that the parties in the original case conducted fraudulent litigation through malicious collusion, the provisions of Article 115 of the Civil Procedure Law shall apply.

<a id="art-191"></a>第一百九十一条　单位有[民事诉讼法第一百一十五条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-115)或者第一百一十六条规定行为的，人民法院应当对该单位进行罚款，并可以对其主要负责人或者直接责任人员予以罚款、拘留；构成犯罪的，依法追究刑事责任。

**Article 191** . Where a unit commits an act specified in Article 115 or Article 116 of the Civil Procedure Law, the People's Court shall impose a fine on the unit, and may also impose a fine or detention on its principal person in charge or directly responsible persons; if the act constitutes a crime, criminal liability shall be investigated in accordance with law.

<a id="art-192"></a>第一百九十二条　有关单位接到人民法院协助执行通知书后，有下列行为之一的，人民法院可以适用[民事诉讼法第一百一十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-117)规定处理：  
(一)允许被执行人高消费的；  
(二)允许被执行人出境的；  
(三)拒不停止办理有关财产权证照转移手续、权属变更登记、规划审批等手续的；  
(四)以需要内部请示、内部审批，有内部规定等为由拖延办理的。

**Article 192** . Where the relevant unit, after receiving a notice of assistance in enforcement from the People's Court, commits any of the following acts, the People's Court may handle the matter in accordance with the provisions of Article 117 of the Civil Procedure Law:  
(1) permitting the person subject to execution to engage in high consumption;  
(2) permitting the person subject to execution to leave the country;  
(3) refusing to cease handling procedures such as transfer of property right certificates, change of ownership registration, and planning approval;  
(4) delaying the handling on the grounds that internal instructions, internal approvals, or internal regulations are required.

<a id="art-193"></a>第一百九十三条　人民法院对个人或者单位采取罚款措施时，应当根据其实施妨害民事诉讼行为的性质、情节、后果，当地的经济发展水平，以及诉讼标的额等因素，在[民事诉讼法第一百一十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-118)第一款规定的限额内确定相应的罚款金额。

**Article 193** . When the People's Court imposes a fine on an individual or a unit, it shall determine the corresponding amount of the fine within the limits prescribed in paragraph 1 of Article 118 of the Civil Procedure Law, taking into account such factors as the nature, circumstances, and consequences of the act obstructing civil litigation, the local level of economic development, and the amount of the subject matter of the action.

### 九、诉讼费用

<a id="art-194"></a>第一百九十四条　依照[民事诉讼法第五十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-57)审理的案件不预交案件受理费，结案后按照诉讼标的额由败诉方交纳。

**Article 194** . Cases tried in accordance with Article 57 of the Civil Procedure Law shall not require prepayment of the case acceptance fee; after the case is concluded, the losing party shall pay the fee according to the amount of the subject matter of the action.

<a id="art-195"></a>第一百九十五条　支付令失效后转入诉讼程序的，债权人应当按照《诉讼费用交纳办法》补交案件受理费。  
支付令被撤销后，债权人另行起诉的，按照《诉讼费用交纳办法》交纳诉讼费用。

**Article 195** . Where a payment order ceases to have effect and the case is transferred to litigation proceedings, the creditor shall pay the balance of the case acceptance fee in accordance with the Measures for the Payment of Litigation Fees.  
Where the payment order is revoked and the creditor brings another action, the litigation fees shall be paid in accordance with the Measures for the Payment of Litigation Fees.

<a id="art-196"></a>第一百九十六条　人民法院改变原判决、裁定、调解结果的，应当在裁判文书中对原审诉讼费用的负担一并作出处理。

**Article 196** . If the People's Court changes the result of the original judgment, ruling, or mediation, it shall, in the adjudicative document, simultaneously deal with the allocation of the litigation fees of the original trial.

<a id="art-197"></a>第一百九十七条　诉讼标的物是证券的，按照证券交易规则并根据当事人起诉之日前最后一个交易日的收盘价、当日的市场价或者其载明的金额计算诉讼标的金额。

**Article 197** . Where the subject matter of the action consists of securities, the amount of the subject matter of the action shall be calculated in accordance with securities trading rules and based on the closing price on the last trading day before the date on which the party brings the action, the market price on the date of bringing the action, or the amount stated therein.

<a id="art-198"></a>第一百九十八条　诉讼标的物是房屋、土地、林木、车辆、船舶、文物等特定物或者知识产权，起诉时价值难以确定的，人民法院应当向原告释明主张过高或者过低的诉讼风险，以原告主张的价值确定诉讼标的金额。

**Article 198** . Where the subject matter of the action is a specific object such as a house, land, trees, vehicle, ship, or cultural relic, or intellectual property, and the value is difficult to determine at the time of bringing the action, the People's Court shall explain to the plaintiff the litigation risks of claiming an excessively high or excessively low value, and determine the amount of the subject matter of the action based on the value claimed by the plaintiff.

<a id="art-199"></a>第一百九十九条　适用简易程序审理的案件转为普通程序的，原告自接到人民法院交纳诉讼费用通知之日起七日内补交案件受理费。  
原告无正当理由未按期足额补交的，按撤诉处理，已经收取的诉讼费用退还一半。

**Article 199** . Where a case tried under the summary procedure is converted to the ordinary procedure, the plaintiff shall pay the balance of the case acceptance fee within seven days from the date of receipt of the notice of payment of litigation fees from the People's Court.  
If the plaintiff fails to pay the full balance within the time limit without justifiable reasons, the case shall be treated as withdrawn, and half of the litigation fees already collected shall be refunded.

<a id="art-200"></a>第二百条　破产程序中有关债务人的民事诉讼案件，按照财产案件标准交纳诉讼费，但劳动争议案件除外。

**Article 200** . Civil action cases concerning the debtor in bankruptcy proceedings shall pay litigation fees in accordance with the standards for property cases, except for labor dispute cases.

<a id="art-201"></a>第二百零一条　既有财产性诉讼请求，又有非财产性诉讼请求的，按照财产性诉讼请求的标准交纳诉讼费。  
有多个财产性诉讼请求的，合并计算交纳诉讼费；诉讼请求中有多个非财产性诉讼请求的，按一件交纳诉讼费。

**Article 201** . Where there are both property claims and non-property claims, the litigation fees shall be paid according to the standard for property claims.  
If there are multiple property claims, the litigation fees shall be paid on a consolidated basis; if there are multiple non-property claims among the claims, the litigation fees shall be paid as for one claim.

<a id="art-202"></a>第二百零二条　原告、被告、第三人分别上诉的，按照上诉请求分别预交二审案件受理费。  
同一方多人共同上诉的，只预交一份二审案件受理费；分别上诉的，按照上诉请求分别预交二审案件受理费。

**Article 202** . Where the plaintiff, defendant, and third party file appeals separately, the case acceptance fee for the second instance shall be prepaid separately according to their respective appellate claims.  
Where multiple persons on the same side jointly appeal, only one second-instance case acceptance fee shall be prepaid; where they file separate appeals, the second-instance case acceptance fees shall be prepaid separately according to their respective appellate claims.

<a id="art-203"></a>第二百零三条　承担连带责任的当事人败诉的，应当共同负担诉讼费用。

**Article 203** . Where parties bearing joint and several liability lose the action, they shall jointly bear the litigation fees.

<a id="art-204"></a>第二百零四条　实现担保物权案件，人民法院裁定拍卖、变卖担保财产的，申请费由债务人、担保人负担；人民法院裁定驳回申请的，申请费由申请人负担。  
申请人另行起诉的，其已经交纳的申请费可以从案件受理费中扣除。

**Article 204** . In a case for realizing a security interest, if the People's Court rules to auction or sell the secured property, the application fee shall be borne by the debtor and the guarantor; if the People's Court rules to reject the application, the application fee shall be borne by the applicant.  
If the applicant subsequently brings another action, the application fee already paid may be deducted from the case acceptance fee.

<a id="art-205"></a>第二百零五条　拍卖、变卖担保财产的裁定作出后，人民法院强制执行的，按照执行金额收取执行申请费。

**Article 205** . After a ruling to auction or sell secured property is made, if the People's Court enforces it compulsorily, an enforcement application fee shall be collected according to the amount enforced.

<a id="art-206"></a>第二百零六条　人民法院决定减半收取案件受理费的，只能减半一次。

**Article 206** . Where the People's Court decides to collect half of the case acceptance fee, the fee may be halved only once.

<a id="art-207"></a>第二百零七条　判决生效后，胜诉方预交但不应负担的诉讼费用，人民法院应当退还，由败诉方向人民法院交纳，但胜诉方自愿承担或者同意败诉方直接向其支付的除外。  
当事人拒不交纳诉讼费用的，人民法院可以强制执行。

**Article 207** . After the judgment takes effect, the People's Court shall refund the litigation fees prepaid by the prevailing party that the prevailing party should not bear, and the losing party shall pay such fees to the People's Court, unless the prevailing party voluntarily bears them or agrees that the losing party may pay them directly to the prevailing party.  
If a party refuses to pay the litigation fees, the People's Court may enforce payment compulsorily.

### 十、第一审普通程序

<a id="art-208"></a>第二百零八条　人民法院接到当事人提交的民事起诉状时，对符合[民事诉讼法第一百二十二条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-122)的规定，且不属于第一百二十七条规定情形的，应当登记立案；对当场不能判定是否符合起诉条件的，应当接收起诉材料，并出具注明收到日期的书面凭证。  
需要补充必要相关材料的，人民法院应当及时告知当事人。在补齐相关材料后，应当在七日内决定是否立案。  
立案后发现不符合起诉条件或者属于[民事诉讼法第一百二十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-127)规定情形的，裁定驳回起诉。

**Article 208** . When the People's Court receives a civil complaint submitted by a party, it shall register and docket the case if the complaint conforms to the provisions of Article 122 of the Civil Procedure Law and does not fall under the circumstances specified in Article 127 thereof. If it cannot determine at that time whether the conditions for filing an action are satisfied, it shall accept the complaint materials and issue a written receipt indicating the date of receipt.  
If necessary relevant materials need to be supplemented, the People's Court shall promptly inform the party. After the relevant materials are supplemented, the People's Court shall decide whether to docket the case within seven days.  
If, after docketing, it is found that the case does not satisfy the conditions for filing an action or falls under the circumstances specified in Article 127 of the Civil Procedure Law, the People's Court shall, by ruling, dismiss the action.

<a id="art-209"></a>第二百零九条　原告提供被告的姓名或者名称、住所等信息具体明确，足以使被告与他人相区别的，可以认定为有明确的被告。  
起诉状列写被告信息不足以认定明确的被告的，人民法院可以告知原告补正。原告补正后仍不能确定明确的被告的，人民法院裁定不予受理。

**Article 209** . If the plaintiff provides the defendant's name or title, domicile, and other information with sufficient specificity to distinguish the defendant from others, the defendant may be deemed to be definite.  
If the information listed for the defendant in the complaint is insufficient to establish a definite defendant, the People's Court may inform the plaintiff to supplement or correct it. If, after supplementation or correction, the defendant still cannot be determined to be definite, the People's Court shall rule that the case is not accepted.

<a id="art-210"></a>第二百一十条　原告在起诉状中有谩骂和人身攻击之辞的，人民法院应当告知其修改后提起诉讼。

**Article 210** . If the plaintiff's complaint contains abusive language or personal attacks, the People's Court shall inform the plaintiff to revise it before bringing the action.

<a id="art-211"></a>第二百一十一条　对本院没有管辖权的案件，告知原告向有管辖权的人民法院起诉；原告坚持起诉的，裁定不予受理；立案后发现本院没有管辖权的，应当将案件移送有管辖权的人民法院。

**Article 211** . For a case over which the court has no jurisdiction, the plaintiff shall be informed to bring the action in a People's Court that has jurisdiction; if the plaintiff insists on bringing the action, the court shall rule not to accept the case; if, after docketing, it is found that the court has no jurisdiction, the case shall be transferred to a People's Court that has jurisdiction.

<a id="art-212"></a>第二百一十二条　裁定不予受理、驳回起诉的案件，原告再次起诉，符合起诉条件且不属于[民事诉讼法第一百二十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-127)规定情形的，人民法院应予受理。

**Article 212** . Where a case has been ruled not to be accepted or the action has been dismissed, and the plaintiff brings the action again, if the action meets the conditions for filing and does not fall under the circumstances specified in Article 127 of the Civil Procedure Law, the People's Court shall accept it.

<a id="art-213"></a>第二百一十三条　原告应当预交而未预交案件受理费，人民法院应当通知其预交，通知后仍不预交或者申请减、缓、免未获批准而仍不预交的，裁定按撤诉处理。

**Article 213** . If the plaintiff should prepay the case acceptance fee but fails to do so, the People's Court shall notify the plaintiff to prepay it. If, after notification, the plaintiff still fails to prepay, or if the application for reduction, deferral, or exemption is not approved and the plaintiff still fails to prepay, the court shall, by ruling, treat the case as withdrawn.

<a id="art-214"></a>第二百一十四条　原告撤诉或者人民法院按撤诉处理后，原告以同一诉讼请求再次起诉的，人民法院应予受理。  
原告撤诉或者按撤诉处理的离婚案件，没有新情况、新理由，六个月内又起诉的，比照[民事诉讼法第一百二十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-127)第七项的规定不予受理。

**Article 214** . If the plaintiff withdraws the action or the action is treated as withdrawn by the People's Court, and the plaintiff brings the action again with the same claim, the People's Court shall accept it.  
For a divorce case in which the plaintiff withdraws the action or the action is treated as withdrawn, if the plaintiff brings the action again within six months without new circumstances or new reasons, the action shall not be accepted by analogy to the provisions of item 7 of Article 127 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-215"></a>第二百一十五条　依照[民事诉讼法第一百二十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-127)第二项的规定，当事人在书面合同中订有仲裁条款，或者在发生纠纷后达成书面仲裁协议，一方向人民法院起诉的，人民法院应当告知原告向仲裁机构申请仲裁，其坚持起诉的，裁定不予受理，但仲裁条款或者仲裁协议不成立、无效、失效、内容不明确无法执行的除外。

**Article 215** . In accordance with item 2 of Article 127 of the Civil Procedure Law, where the parties have agreed on an arbitration clause in a written contract or have reached a written arbitration agreement after the dispute arises, and one party brings an action in the People's Court, the People's Court shall inform the plaintiff to apply to an arbitration institution for arbitration; if the plaintiff insists on bringing the action, the court shall rule not to accept it, except where the arbitration clause or arbitration agreement is not formed, is invalid, has ceased to have effect, or its content is unclear and cannot be performed.

<a id="art-216"></a>第二百一十六条　在人民法院首次开庭前，被告以有书面仲裁协议为由对受理民事案件提出异议的，人民法院应当进行审查。  
经审查符合下列情形之一的，人民法院应当裁定驳回起诉：  
(一)仲裁机构或者人民法院已经确认仲裁协议有效的；  
(二)当事人没有在仲裁庭首次开庭前对仲裁协议的效力提出异议的；  
(三)仲裁协议符合[仲裁法第十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/律师、仲裁与公证/中华人民共和国仲裁法.md#art-16)规定且不具有[仲裁法第十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/律师、仲裁与公证/中华人民共和国仲裁法.md#art-17)规定情形的。

**Article 216** . Before the first hearing in the People's Court, if the defendant raises an objection to the acceptance of a civil case on the ground that there is a written arbitration agreement, the People's Court shall examine the matter.  
If, upon examination, any of the following circumstances exists, the People's Court shall, by ruling, dismiss the action:  
(1) the arbitration institution or the People's Court has already confirmed that the arbitration agreement is valid;  
(2) the parties have not raised an objection to the validity of the arbitration agreement before the first hearing of the arbitral tribunal;  
(3) the arbitration agreement conforms to the provisions of Article 16 of the Arbitration Law and does not fall under the circumstances specified in Article 17 of the Arbitration Law.

<a id="art-217"></a>第二百一十七条　夫妻一方下落不明，另一方诉至人民法院，只要求离婚，不申请宣告下落不明人失踪或者死亡的案件，人民法院应当受理，对下落不明人公告送达诉讼文书。

**Article 217** . Where one spouse is missing and the other spouse brings an action in the People's Court only for divorce, without applying for a declaration of disappearance or death of the missing person, the People's Court shall accept the case and serve the litigation documents on the missing person by public announcement.

<a id="art-218"></a>第二百一十八条　赡养费、扶养费、抚养费案件，裁判发生法律效力后，因新情况、新理由，一方当事人再行起诉要求增加或者减少费用的，人民法院应作为新案受理。

**Article 218** . For cases involving alimony, maintenance, and child support, after the judgment or ruling takes legal effect, if a party brings another action to increase or decrease the amount due to new circumstances or new reasons, the People's Court shall accept it as a new case.

<a id="art-219"></a>第二百一十九条　当事人超过诉讼时效期间起诉的，人民法院应予受理。受理后对方当事人提出诉讼时效抗辩，人民法院经审理认为抗辩事由成立的，判决驳回原告的诉讼请求。

**Article 219** . If a party brings an action after the period of limitation of action has expired, the People's Court shall accept it. If, after acceptance, the other party raises a defense of limitation of action, and the People's Court finds upon trial that the defense is established, it shall, by judgment, reject the plaintiff's claims.

<a id="art-220"></a>第二百二十条　[民事诉讼法第七十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-71)、第一百三十七条、第一百五十九条规定的商业秘密，是指生产工艺、配方、贸易联系、购销渠道等当事人不愿公开的技术秘密、商业情报及信息。

**Article 220** . The trade secrets as provided in Articles 71, 137, and 159 of the Civil Procedure Law refer to technical secrets, commercial intelligence, and information, such as production processes, formulas, trade contacts, and purchase and sales channels, that the parties are unwilling to disclose.

<a id="art-221"></a>第二百二十一条　基于同一事实发生的纠纷，当事人分别向同一人民法院起诉的，人民法院可以合并审理。

**Article 221** : Where disputes arising from the same facts are prosecuted by the parties separately in the same People's Court, the People's Court may consolidate the trials.

<a id="art-222"></a>第二百二十二条　原告在起诉状中直接列写第三人的，视为其申请人民法院追加该第三人参加诉讼。是否通知第三人参加诉讼，由人民法院审查决定。

**Article 222** : Where the plaintiff directly lists a third party in the complaint, it shall be deemed that the plaintiff applies to the People's Court for adding such third party to participate in the action. Whether to notify the third party to participate in the action shall be determined by the People's Court upon examination.

<a id="art-223"></a>第二百二十三条　当事人在提交答辩状期间提出管辖异议，又针对起诉状的内容进行答辩的，人民法院应当依照[民事诉讼法第一百三十条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-130)第一款的规定，对管辖异议进行审查。  
当事人未提出管辖异议，就案件实体内容进行答辩、陈述或者反诉的，可以认定为[民事诉讼法第一百三十条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-130)第二款规定的应诉答辩。

**Article 223** : Where a party raises an objection to jurisdiction during the period for submitting its statement of defense and also answers the contents of the complaint, the People's Court shall, in accordance with the provisions of Article 130, Paragraph 1 of the Civil Procedure Law, examine the objection to jurisdiction.  
Where a party, without raising an objection to jurisdiction, answers, makes statements, or files a counterclaim on the substantive contents of the case, it may be determined as responding to the action as provided in Article 130, Paragraph 2 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-224"></a>第二百二十四条　依照[民事诉讼法第一百三十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-136)第四项规定，人民法院可以在答辩期届满后，通过组织证据交换、召集庭前会议等方式，作好审理前的准备。

**Article 224** : In accordance with Item (4) of Article 136 of the Civil Procedure Law, the People's Court may, after the expiration of the defense period, make preparations for trial by organizing evidence exchange, convening pre-trial conferences, or other means.

<a id="art-225"></a>第二百二十五条　根据案件具体情况，庭前会议可以包括下列内容：  
(一)明确原告的诉讼请求和被告的答辩意见；  
(二)审查处理当事人增加、变更诉讼请求的申请和提出的反诉，以及第三人提出的与本案有关的诉讼请求；  
(三)根据当事人的申请决定调查收集证据，委托鉴定，要求当事人提供证据，进行勘验，进行证据保全；  
(四)组织交换证据；  
(五)归纳争议焦点；  
(六)进行调解。

**Article 225** : Depending on the specific circumstances of the case, the pre-trial conference may include the following contents:  
(1) clarifying the plaintiff's claims and the defendant's defense opinions;  
(2) examining and handling the parties' applications to add or modify claims and the counterclaim filed, as well as claims related to the case raised by a third party;  
(3) deciding, upon the parties' application, to investigate and collect evidence, entrust expert evaluation, require the parties to provide evidence, conduct inspection, and preserve evidence;  
(4) organizing evidence exchange;  
(5) summarizing the disputed issues;  
(6) conducting mediation.

<a id="art-226"></a>第二百二十六条　人民法院应当根据当事人的诉讼请求、答辩意见以及证据交换的情况，归纳争议焦点，并就归纳的争议焦点征求当事人的意见。

**Article 226** : The People's Court shall, based on the parties' claims, defense opinions, and the circumstances of evidence exchange, summarize the disputed issues and seek the parties' opinions on the summarized disputed issues.

<a id="art-227"></a>第二百二十七条　人民法院适用普通程序审理案件，应当在开庭三日前用传票传唤当事人。对诉讼代理人、证人、鉴定人、勘验人、翻译人员应当用通知书通知其到庭。当事人或者其他诉讼参与人在外地的，应当留有必要的在途时间。

**Article 227** : Where the People's Court applies the ordinary procedure to try a case, it shall summon the parties by summons three days before the hearing. Litigation agents, witnesses, expert evaluators, inspectors, and interpreters shall be notified to appear in court by written notice. Where the parties or other litigation participants are in other places, necessary travel time shall be reserved.

<a id="art-228"></a>第二百二十八条　法庭审理应当围绕当事人争议的事实、证据和法律适用等焦点问题进行。

**Article 228** : The court trial shall focus on issues such as the facts, evidence, and application of law disputed by the parties.

<a id="art-229"></a>第二百二十九条　当事人在庭审中对其在审理前的准备阶段认可的事实和证据提出不同意见的，人民法院应当责令其说明理由。必要时，可以责令其提供相应证据。人民法院应当结合当事人的诉讼能力、证据和案件的具体情况进行审查。理由成立的，可以列入争议焦点进行审理。

**Article 229** : Where a party raises differing opinions during the court trial regarding facts and evidence that it had admitted during the pre-trial preparation stage, the People's Court shall order it to state the reasons. When necessary, the court may order it to provide corresponding evidence. The People's Court shall review the matter by taking into account the parties' litigation capability, the evidence, and the specific circumstances of the case. If the reasons are established, the matter may be included in the disputed issues for trial.

<a id="art-230"></a>第二百三十条　人民法院根据案件具体情况并征得当事人同意，可以将法庭调查和法庭辩论合并进行。

**Article 230** : The People's Court may, based on the specific circumstances of the case and with the consent of the parties, merge the court investigation and court debate.

<a id="art-231"></a>第二百三十一条　当事人在法庭上提出新的证据的，人民法院应当依照[民事诉讼法第六十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-68)第二款规定和本解释相关规定处理。

**Article 231** : Where a party submits new evidence in court, the People's Court shall handle it in accordance with the provisions of Article 68, Paragraph 2 of the Civil Procedure Law and the relevant provisions of this Interpretation.

<a id="art-232"></a>第二百三十二条　在案件受理后，法庭辩论结束前，原告增加诉讼请求，被告提出反诉，第三人提出与本案有关的诉讼请求，可以合并审理的，人民法院应当合并审理。

**Article 232** : Where, after acceptance of the case and before the conclusion of court debate, the plaintiff adds claims, the defendant files a counterclaim, or a third party raises claims related to the case, and the claims may be tried jointly, the People's Court shall try them jointly.

<a id="art-233"></a>第二百三十三条　反诉的当事人应当限于本诉的当事人的范围。  
反诉与本诉的诉讼请求基于相同法律关系、诉讼请求之间具有因果关系，或者反诉与本诉的诉讼请求基于相同事实的，人民法院应当合并审理。  
反诉应由其他人民法院专属管辖，或者与本诉的诉讼标的及诉讼请求所依据的事实、理由无关联的，裁定不予受理，告知另行起诉。

**Article 233** : Entities of a counterclaim shall be limited to the scope of the parties to the principal action.  
Where the counterclaim and the principal action's claims are based on the same legal relationship, the claims have a causal relationship, or the counterclaim and the principal action's claims are based on the same facts, the People's Court shall try them jointly.  
Where the counterclaim shall be under the exclusive jurisdiction of another People's Court, or is not related to the subject matter of the principal action and the facts and reasons on which the claims are based, the People's Court shall rule not to accept it and inform the party to file a separate action.

<a id="art-234"></a>第二百三十四条　无民事行为能力人的离婚诉讼，当事人的法定代理人应当到庭；法定代理人不能到庭的，人民法院应当在查清事实的基础上，依法作出判决。

**Article 234** : In a divorce action of a person without capacity for civil conduct, the party's legal representative shall appear in court. If the legal representative cannot appear in court, the People's Court shall, on the basis of ascertaining the facts, render a judgment according to law.

<a id="art-235"></a>第二百三十五条　无民事行为能力的当事人的法定代理人，经传票传唤无正当理由拒不到庭，属于原告方的，比照[民事诉讼法第一百四十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-146)的规定，按撤诉处理；属于被告方的，比照[民事诉讼法第一百四十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-147)的规定，缺席判决。必要时，人民法院可以拘传其到庭。

**Article 235** . Where a statutory agent of a party without capacity for civil conduct, after being served with a summons, refuses to appear in court without justifiable reasons, if the party belongs to the plaintiff side, the case shall be treated as withdrawal of the action by reference to Article 146 of the Civil Procedure Law; if the party belongs to the defendant side, a default judgment shall be rendered by reference to Article 147 of the Civil Procedure Law. If necessary, the People's Court may compel the agent to appear in court.

<a id="art-236"></a>第二百三十六条　有独立请求权的第三人经人民法院传票传唤，无正当理由拒不到庭的，或者未经法庭许可中途退庭的，比照[民事诉讼法第一百四十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-146)的规定，按撤诉处理。

**Article 236** . Where a third party with independent claims, after being served with a summons by the People's Court, refuses to appear in court without justifiable reasons, or leaves the court midway without permission of the court, the case shall be treated as withdrawal of the action by reference to Article 146 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-237"></a>第二百三十七条　有独立请求权的第三人参加诉讼后，原告申请撤诉，人民法院在准许原告撤诉后，有独立请求权的第三人作为另案原告，原案原告、被告作为另案被告，诉讼继续进行。

**Article 237** . After a third party with independent claims participates in the proceedings, if the plaintiff applies to withdraw the action and the People's Court permits the withdrawal, the third party with independent claims shall act as the plaintiff in a separate case, and the plaintiff and defendant in the original case shall act as the defendants in the separate case, and the proceedings shall continue.

<a id="art-238"></a>第二百三十八条　当事人申请撤诉或者依法可以按撤诉处理的案件，如果当事人有违反法律的行为需要依法处理的，人民法院可以不准许撤诉或者不按撤诉处理。  
法庭辩论终结后原告申请撤诉，被告不同意的，人民法院可以不予准许。

**Article 238** . In a case where a party applies to withdraw the action or the case may be treated as withdrawal of the action according to law, if the party has committed acts in violation of law that need to be handled according to law, the People's Court may refuse to permit the withdrawal or refuse to treat the case as withdrawal of the action.  
After the conclusion of court debate, if the plaintiff applies to withdraw the action and the defendant does not consent, the People's Court may refuse to permit it.

<a id="art-239"></a>第二百三十九条　人民法院准许本诉原告撤诉的，应当对反诉继续审理；被告申请撤回反诉的，人民法院应予准许。

**Article 239** . Where the People's Court permits the plaintiff in the principal action to withdraw the action, it shall continue to hear the counterclaim; where the defendant applies to withdraw the counterclaim, the People's Court shall permit it.

<a id="art-240"></a>第二百四十条　无独立请求权的第三人经人民法院传票传唤，无正当理由拒不到庭，或者未经法庭许可中途退庭的，不影响案件的审理。

**Article 240** . The failure of a third party without independent claims to appear in court without justifiable reasons after being served with a summons by the People's Court, or to leave the court midway without permission of the court, shall not affect the hearing of the case.

<a id="art-241"></a>第二百四十一条　被告经传票传唤无正当理由拒不到庭，或者未经法庭许可中途退庭的，人民法院应当按期开庭或者继续开庭审理，对到庭的当事人诉讼请求、双方的诉辩理由以及已经提交的证据及其他诉讼材料进行审理后，可以依法缺席判决。

**Article 241** . Where the defendant, after being served with a summons, refuses to appear in court without justifiable reasons, or leaves the court midway without permission of the court, the People's Court shall hold the hearing on schedule or continue the hearing, and after hearing the claims of the parties present, the arguments of both parties, and the evidence and other litigation materials already submitted, it may render a default judgment according to law.

<a id="art-242"></a>第二百四十二条　一审宣判后，原审人民法院发现判决有错误，当事人在上诉期内提出上诉的，原审人民法院可以提出原判决有错误的意见，报送第二审人民法院，由第二审人民法院按照第二审程序进行审理；当事人不上诉的，按照审判监督程序处理。

**Article 242** . After a first-instance judgment is pronounced, if the original trial People's Court discovers that the judgment contains an error and the parties file an appeal within the appeal period, the original trial People's Court may state its opinion that the original judgment contains an error and submit it to the second-instance People's Court, which shall try the case under the second-instance procedure; if the parties do not appeal, the case shall be handled under the trial supervision procedure.

<a id="art-243"></a>第二百四十三条　[民事诉讼法第一百五十二条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-152)规定的审限，是指从立案之日起至裁判宣告、调解书送达之日止的期间，但公告期间、鉴定期间、双方当事人和解期间、审理当事人提出的管辖异议以及处理人民法院之间的管辖争议期间不应计算在内。

**Article 243** . The trial period provided in Article 152 of the Civil Procedure Law refers to the period from the date of filing the case to the date on which the judgment or ruling is pronounced and the mediation statement is served, provided that the periods for public announcement, expert evaluation, reconciliation between the parties, hearing a jurisdiction objection raised by a party, and handling a jurisdiction dispute between People's Courts shall not be included.

<a id="art-244"></a>第二百四十四条　可以上诉的判决书、裁定书不能同时送达双方当事人的，上诉期从各自收到判决书、裁定书之日计算。

**Article 244** . Where a judgment or ruling that may be appealed cannot be served on both parties simultaneously, the appeal period shall be calculated from the date on which each party receives the judgment or ruling.

<a id="art-245"></a>第二百四十五条　[民事诉讼法第一百五十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-157)第一款第七项规定的笔误是指法律文书误写、误算，诉讼费用漏写、误算和其他笔误。

**Article 245** . The clerical error provided in Item (7) of Paragraph 1 of Article 157 of the Civil Procedure Law refers to erroneous writing or erroneous calculation in legal documents, omission or erroneous calculation of litigation costs, and other clerical errors.

<a id="art-246"></a>第二百四十六条　裁定中止诉讼的原因消除，恢复诉讼程序时，不必撤销原裁定，从人民法院通知或者准许当事人双方继续进行诉讼时起，中止诉讼的裁定即失去效力。

**Article 246** . When the cause for the ruling to suspend the action is eliminated and the proceedings are resumed, it is not necessary to revoke the original ruling. From the time when the People's Court notifies or permits both parties to continue the proceedings, the ruling to suspend the action shall cease to have effect.

<a id="art-247"></a>第二百四十七条　当事人就已经提起诉讼的事项在诉讼过程中或者裁判生效后再次起诉，同时符合下列条件的，构成重复起诉：  
(一)后诉与前诉的当事人相同；  
(二)后诉与前诉的诉讼标的相同；  
(三)后诉与前诉的诉讼请求相同，或者后诉的诉讼请求实质上否定前诉裁判结果。  
当事人重复起诉的，裁定不予受理；已经受理的，裁定驳回起诉，但法律、司法解释另有规定的除外。

**Article 247** . Where a party files another action on a matter for which an action has already been instituted during the proceedings or after the judgment or ruling takes legal effect, and the following conditions are concurrently met, it constitutes a duplicate action:  
(1) the parties in the subsequent action are the same as those in the previous action;  
(2) the subject matter of the subsequent action is the same as that of the previous action;  
(3) the claims in the subsequent action are the same as those in the previous action, or the claims in the subsequent action substantially negate the adjudication result of the previous action.  
Where a party files a duplicate action, the People's Court shall rule not to accept it; if it has already been accepted, it shall rule to dismiss the action, except as otherwise provided by law or judicial interpretation.

<a id="art-248"></a>第二百四十八条　裁判发生法律效力后，发生新的事实，当事人再次提起诉讼的，人民法院应当依法受理。

**Article 248** . After a judgment or ruling takes legal effect, if new facts arise and a party brings an action again, the People's Court shall accept the action according to law.

<a id="art-249"></a>第二百四十九条　在诉讼中，争议的民事权利义务转移的，不影响当事人的诉讼主体资格和诉讼地位。人民法院作出的发生法律效力的判决、裁定对受让人具有拘束力。  
受让人申请以无独立请求权的第三人身份参加诉讼的，人民法院可予准许。受让人申请替代当事人承担诉讼的，人民法院可以根据案件的具体情况决定是否准许；不予准许的，可以追加其为无独立请求权的第三人。

**Article 249** . In the course of litigation, if the disputed civil rights and obligations are transferred, the party's litigation subject qualification and litigation status shall not be affected. The legally effective judgments and rulings made by the People's Court shall be binding on the transferee.  
Where the transferee applies to participate in the proceedings as a third party without independent claims, the People's Court may permit it. Where the transferee applies to replace the party and assume the litigation, the People's Court may decide whether to permit it based on the specific circumstances of the case; if it does not permit it, the transferee may be added as a third party without independent claims.

<a id="art-250"></a>第二百五十条　依照[本解释第二百四十九条](#art-249)规定，人民法院准许受让人替代当事人承担诉讼的，裁定变更当事人。  
变更当事人后，诉讼程序以受让人为当事人继续进行，原当事人应当退出诉讼。原当事人已经完成的诉讼行为对受让人具有拘束力。

**Article 250** . Where, in accordance with Article 249 of this Interpretation, the People's Court permits the transferee to replace the party and assume the litigation, it shall rule to change the party.  
After the change of party, the proceedings shall continue with the transferee as the party, and the original party shall withdraw from the proceedings. The litigation acts already completed by the original party shall be binding on the transferee.

<a id="art-251"></a>第二百五十一条　二审裁定撤销一审判决发回重审的案件，当事人申请变更、增加诉讼请求或者提出反诉，第三人提出与本案有关的诉讼请求的，依照[民事诉讼法第一百四十三条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-143)规定处理。

**Article 251** . For a case in which a second-instance ruling revokes the first-instance judgment and remands the case for retrial, if a party applies to modify or add claims or file a counterclaim, or a third party raises a claim related to the case, the case shall be handled in accordance with Article 143 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-252"></a>第二百五十二条　再审裁定撤销原判决、裁定发回重审的案件，当事人申请变更、增加诉讼请求或者提出反诉，符合下列情形之一的，人民法院应当准许：  
(一)原审未合法传唤缺席判决，影响当事人行使诉讼权利的；  
(二)追加新的诉讼当事人的；  
(三)诉讼标的物灭失或者发生变化致使原诉讼请求无法实现的；  
(四)当事人申请变更、增加的诉讼请求或者提出的反诉，无法通过另诉解决的。

**Article 252** . For a case in which a retrial ruling revokes the original judgment or ruling and remands the case for retrial, the People's Court shall permit a party to modify or add claims or file a counterclaim in any of the following circumstances:  
(1) the original trial rendered a default judgment without lawful summons, affecting the party's exercise of litigation rights;  
(2) new parties to the action are added;  
(3) the subject matter of the action is destroyed or changed, making the original claims impossible to realize;  
(4) the claims that the party applies to modify or add or the counterclaim filed cannot be resolved through a separate action.

<a id="art-253"></a>第二百五十三条　当庭宣判的案件，除当事人当庭要求邮寄发送裁判文书的外，人民法院应当告知当事人或者诉讼代理人领取裁判文书的时间和地点以及逾期不领取的法律后果。上述情况，应当记入笔录。

**Article 253** . For a case in which judgment is pronounced in court, except where a party requests at the court session that the adjudication document be sent by mail, the People's Court shall inform the parties or their litigation agents of the time and place for collecting the adjudication document and the legal consequences of failing to collect it within the prescribed time. The above circumstances shall be recorded in the record.

<a id="art-254"></a>第二百五十四条　公民、法人或者其他组织申请查阅发生法律效力的判决书、裁定书的，应当向作出该生效裁判的人民法院提出。申请应当以书面形式提出，并提供具体的案号或者当事人姓名、名称。

**Article 254** . Where a citizen, legal person, or other organization applies to inspect a legally effective judgment or ruling, it shall apply to the People's Court that rendered the effective adjudication. The application shall be made in writing and shall provide the specific case number or the name of the party.

<a id="art-255"></a>第二百五十五条　对于查阅判决书、裁定书的申请，人民法院根据下列情形分别处理：  
(一)判决书、裁定书已经通过信息网络向社会公开的，应当引导申请人自行查阅；  
(二)判决书、裁定书未通过信息网络向社会公开，且申请符合要求的，应当及时提供便捷的查阅服务；  
(三)判决书、裁定书尚未发生法律效力，或者已失去法律效力的，不提供查阅并告知申请人；  
(四)发生法律效力的判决书、裁定书不是本院作出的，应当告知申请人向作出生效裁判的人民法院申请查阅；  
(五)申请查阅的内容涉及国家秘密、商业秘密、个人隐私的，不予准许并告知申请人。

**Article 255** . For an application to inspect a judgment or ruling, the People's Court shall handle it separately according to the following circumstances:  
(1) if the judgment or ruling has already been disclosed to the public through the information network, the applicant shall be guided to inspect it on its own;  
(2) if the judgment or ruling has not been disclosed to the public through the information network and the application meets the requirements, convenient inspection services shall be provided in a timely manner;  
(3) if the judgment or ruling has not yet taken legal effect or has lost legal effect, inspection shall not be provided and the applicant shall be so informed;  
(4) if the legally effective judgment or ruling was not rendered by this court, the applicant shall be informed to apply to the People's Court that rendered the effective adjudication for inspection;  
(5) if the content requested for inspection involves state secrets, trade secrets, or personal privacy, the application shall not be permitted and the applicant shall be so informed.

### 十一、简易程序

<a id="art-256"></a>第二百五十六条　[民事诉讼法第一百六十条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-160)规定的简单民事案件中的事实清楚，是指当事人对争议的事实陈述基本一致，并能提供相应的证据，无须人民法院调查收集证据即可查明事实；权利义务关系明确是指能明确区分谁是责任的承担者，谁是权利的享有者；争议不大是指当事人对案件的是非、责任承担以及诉讼标的争执无原则分歧。

**Article 256** . 'Clear facts' in a simple civil case as provided in Article 160 of the Civil Procedure Law means that the parties' statements on the disputed facts are basically consistent and they can provide corresponding evidence, so that the facts can be ascertained without the People's Court's investigation and collection of evidence; 'clear rights-and-obligations relationship' means that it can be clearly distinguished who bears liability and who enjoys rights; 'minor dispute' means that the parties have no principled difference over the merits of the case, the bearing of liability, and the subject matter of the action.

<a id="art-257"></a>第二百五十七条　下列案件，不适用简易程序：  
(一)起诉时被告下落不明的；  
(二)发回重审的；  
(三)当事人一方人数众多的；  
(四)适用审判监督程序的；  
(五)涉及国家利益、社会公共利益的；  
(六)第三人起诉请求改变或者撤销生效判决、裁定、调解书的；  
(七)其他不宜适用简易程序的案件。

**Article 257** . The following cases shall not be tried under the summary procedure:  
(1) where the whereabouts of the defendant are unknown at the time of filing the action;  
(2) cases remanded for retrial;  
(3) cases where one party consists of a large number of persons;  
(4) cases under the trial supervision procedure;  
(5) cases involving state interests or social public interests;  
(6) cases where a third party files an action requesting modification or revocation of an effective judgment, ruling, or mediation statement;  
(7) other cases not suitable for the summary procedure.

<a id="art-258"></a>第二百五十八条　适用简易程序审理的案件，审理期限到期后，有特殊情况需要延长的，经本院院长批准，可以延长审理期限。延长后的审理期限累计不得超过四个月。  
人民法院发现案件不宜适用简易程序，需要转为普通程序审理的，应当在审理期限届满前作出裁定并将审判人员及相关事项书面通知双方当事人。  
案件转为普通程序审理的，审理期限自人民法院立案之日计算。

**Article 258** . For a case tried under the summary procedure, if the trial period expires and an extension is needed due to special circumstances, the trial period may be extended with the approval of the president of this court. The cumulative trial period after extension shall not exceed four months.  
Where the People's Court finds that a case is not suitable for the summary procedure and needs to be converted to the ordinary procedure for trial, it shall issue a ruling before the expiration of the trial period and notify both parties in writing of the adjudicators and related matters.  
Where a case is converted to the ordinary procedure for trial, the trial period shall be calculated from the date on which the People's Court docketed the case.

<a id="art-259"></a>第二百五十九条　当事人双方可就开庭方式向人民法院提出申请，由人民法院决定是否准许。经当事人双方同意，可以采用视听传输技术等方式开庭。

**Article 259** . Both parties may submit an application to the People's Court regarding the method of holding the hearing, and the People's Court shall decide whether to permit it. With the consent of both parties, the hearing may be held by means such as audiovisual transmission technology.

<a id="art-260"></a>第二百六十条　已经按照普通程序审理的案件，在开庭后不得转为简易程序审理。

**Article 260** . A case that has already been tried under the ordinary procedure shall not be converted to the summary procedure for trial after the hearing has commenced.

<a id="art-261"></a>第二百六十一条　适用简易程序审理案件，人民法院可以依照[民事诉讼法第九十条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-90)、第一百六十二条的规定采取捎口信、电话、短信、传真、电子邮件等简便方式传唤双方当事人、通知证人和送达诉讼文书。  
以简便方式送达的开庭通知，未经当事人确认或者没有其他证据证明当事人已经收到的，人民法院不得缺席判决。  
适用简易程序审理案件，由审判员独任审判，书记员担任记录。

**Article 261** . In trying a case under the summary procedure, the People's Court may, in accordance with Articles 90 and 162 of the Civil Procedure Law, use simple methods such as passing messages, telephone, text message, fax, or e-mail to summon both parties, notify witnesses, and serve litigation documents.  
Where a notice of hearing is served by simple means, the People's Court shall not render a default judgment unless the notice has been confirmed by the party or there is other evidence proving that the party has received it.  
For a case tried under the summary procedure, a judge shall hear the case alone, and a court clerk shall keep the record.

<a id="art-262"></a>第二百六十二条　人民法庭制作的判决书、裁定书、调解书，必须加盖基层人民法院印章，不得用人民法庭的印章代替基层人民法院的印章。

**Article 262** . Judgments, rulings, and mediation statements produced by a people's tribunal must be affixed with the seal of the Primary People's Court, and the seal of the people's tribunal may not be used in place of the seal of the Primary People's Court.

<a id="art-263"></a>第二百六十三条　适用简易程序审理案件，卷宗中应当具备以下材料：  
(一)起诉状或者口头起诉笔录；  
(二)答辩状或者口头答辩笔录；  
(三)当事人身份证明材料；  
(四)委托他人代理诉讼的授权委托书或者口头委托笔录；  
(五)证据；  
(六)询问当事人笔录；  
(七)审理(包括调解)笔录；  
(八)判决书、裁定书、调解书或者调解协议；  
(九)送达和宣判笔录；  
(十)执行情况；  
(十一)诉讼费收据；  
(十二)适用[民事诉讼法第一百六十五条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-165)规定审理的，有关程序适用的书面告知。

**Article 263** . For a case tried under the summary procedure, the case file shall contain the following materials:  
(1) the complaint or the record of oral complaint;  
(2) the statement of defense or the record of oral defense;  
(3) the identification materials of the parties;  
(4) the power of attorney for a person entrusted to act as agent in the litigation, or the record of oral entrustment;  
(5) evidence;  
(6) records of questioning the parties;  
(7) records of the hearing (including mediation);  
(8) judgments, rulings, mediation statements, or mediation agreements;  
(9) records of service and pronouncement;  
(10) the status of enforcement;  
(11) receipts of litigation costs;  
(12) where the case is tried in accordance with Article 165 of the Civil Procedure Law, a written notice concerning the application of the relevant procedure.

<a id="art-264"></a>第二百六十四条　当事人双方根据[民事诉讼法第一百六十条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-160)第二款规定约定适用简易程序的，应当在开庭前提出。口头提出的，记入笔录，由双方当事人签名或者捺印确认。  
[本解释第二百五十七条](#art-257)规定的案件，当事人约定适用简易程序的，人民法院不予准许。

**Article 264** . Where both parties agree to apply the summary procedure in accordance with Paragraph 2 of Article 160 of the Civil Procedure Law, the agreement shall be proposed before the hearing. If proposed orally, it shall be recorded in the record and confirmed by the signatures or fingerprints of both parties.  
For cases specified in Article 257 of this Interpretation, if the parties agree to apply the summary procedure, the People's Court shall not permit it.

<a id="art-265"></a>第二百六十五条　原告口头起诉的，人民法院应当将当事人的姓名、性别、工作单位、住所、联系方式等基本信息，诉讼请求，事实及理由等准确记入笔录，由原告核对无误后签名或者捺印。对当事人提交的证据材料，应当出具收据。

**Article 265** . Where the plaintiff files the action orally, the People's Court shall accurately record in the record the party's basic information such as name, gender, work unit, domicile, and contact information, as well as the claims, facts, and reasons, and the plaintiff shall sign or affix fingerprints after verifying that it is correct. For the evidentiary materials submitted by the party, a receipt shall be issued.

<a id="art-266"></a>第二百六十六条　适用简易程序案件的举证期限由人民法院确定，也可以由当事人协商一致并经人民法院准许，但不得超过十五日。被告要求书面答辩的，人民法院可在征得其同意的基础上，合理确定答辩期间。  
人民法院应当将举证期限和开庭日期告知双方当事人，并向当事人说明逾期举证以及拒不到庭的法律后果，由双方当事人在笔录和开庭传票的送达回证上签名或者捺印。  
当事人双方均表示不需要举证期限、答辩期间的，人民法院可以立即开庭审理或者确定开庭日期。

**Article 266** .
