For a case under the summary procedure, the time limit for producing evidence shall be determined by the People's Court, or may be agreed upon by both parties with the approval of the People's Court, but shall not exceed fifteen days. Where the defendant requests a written defense, the People's Court may reasonably determine the defense period based on obtaining the defendant's consent.  
The People's Court shall inform both parties of the time limit for producing evidence and the hearing date, and explain to the parties the legal consequences of producing evidence beyond the time limit and of failing to appear in court. Both parties shall sign or affix fingerprints on the record and the proof of service of the hearing summons.  
Where both parties state that they do not need a time limit for producing evidence or a defense period, the People's Court may hold the hearing immediately or determine a hearing date.

<a id="art-267"></a>第二百六十七条　适用简易程序审理案件，可以简便方式进行审理前的准备。

**Article 267** . For a case tried under the summary procedure, preparation before trial may be conducted in a simple manner.

<a id="art-268"></a>第二百六十八条　对没有委托律师、基层法律服务工作者代理诉讼的当事人，人民法院在庭审过程中可以对回避、自认、举证证明责任等相关内容向其作必要的解释或者说明，并在庭审过程中适当提示当事人正确行使诉讼权利、履行诉讼义务。

**Article 268** . With respect to a party who has not entrusted a lawyer or a basic-level legal service worker to act as agent in the litigation, the People's Court may, during the court hearing, give necessary explanations or clarifications on relevant matters such as challenge, admission, and burden of proof, and appropriately remind the party during the hearing to correctly exercise litigation rights and perform litigation obligations.

<a id="art-269"></a>第二百六十九条　当事人就案件适用简易程序提出异议，人民法院经审查，异议成立的，裁定转为普通程序；异议不成立的，裁定驳回。裁定以口头方式作出的，应当记入笔录。  
转为普通程序的，人民法院应当将审判人员及相关事项以书面形式通知双方当事人。  
转为普通程序前，双方当事人已确认的事实，可以不再进行举证、质证。

**Article 269** . Where a party raises an objection to the application of the summary procedure to the case, the People's Court shall, upon examination, rule to convert the case to the ordinary procedure if the objection is established; if the objection is not established, it shall rule to reject it. If the ruling is made orally, it shall be recorded in the record.  
Where the case is converted to the ordinary procedure, the People's Court shall notify both parties in writing of the adjudicators and related matters.  
Facts already confirmed by both parties before the conversion to the ordinary procedure need not be subject to further adduction of evidence or cross-examination.

<a id="art-270"></a>第二百七十条　适用简易程序审理的案件，有下列情形之一的，人民法院在制作判决书、裁定书、调解书时，对认定事实或者裁判理由部分可以适当简化：  
(一)当事人达成调解协议并需要制作民事调解书的；  
(二)一方当事人明确表示承认对方全部或者部分诉讼请求的；  
(三)涉及商业秘密、个人隐私的案件，当事人一方要求简化裁判文书中的相关内容，人民法院认为理由正当的；  
(四)当事人双方同意简化的。

**Article 270** . For a case tried under the summary procedure, where any of the following circumstances exists, the People's Court may appropriately simplify the part on factual findings or adjudication reasons when producing a judgment, ruling, or mediation statement:  
(1) the parties reach a mediation agreement and a civil mediation statement needs to be produced;  
(2) one party expressly admits all or part of the claims of the other party;  
(3) in a case involving trade secrets or personal privacy, one party requests simplification of the relevant contents in the adjudication document and the People's Court considers the reasons justified;  
(4) both parties agree to simplification.

### 十二、简易程序中的小额诉讼

<a id="art-271"></a>第二百七十一条　人民法院审理小额诉讼案件，适用[民事诉讼法第一百六十五条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-165)的规定，实行一审终审。

**Article 271** . When the People's Court tries a small-claim case, the provisions of Article 165 of the Civil Procedure Law shall apply, and the first-instance judgment shall be final.

<a id="art-272"></a>第二百七十二条　[民事诉讼法第一百六十五条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-165)规定的各省、自治区、直辖市上年度就业人员年平均工资，是指已经公布的各省、自治区、直辖市上一年度就业人员年平均工资。在上一年度就业人员年平均工资公布前，以已经公布的最近年度就业人员年平均工资为准。

**Article 272** . The average annual wage of employed persons in the previous year of each province, autonomous region, or municipality directly under the Central Government as provided in Article 165 of the Civil Procedure Law refers to the published average annual wage of employed persons of the previous year in each province, autonomous region, or municipality directly under the Central Government. Before the average annual wage of employed persons of the previous year is published, the average annual wage of employed persons of the latest published year shall prevail.

<a id="art-273"></a>第二百七十三条　海事法院可以适用小额诉讼的程序审理海事、海商案件。案件标的额应当以实际受理案件的海事法院或者其派出法庭所在的省、自治区、直辖市上年度就业人员年平均工资为基数计算。

**Article 273** . A maritime court may try maritime and admiralty cases under the small-claim procedure. The amount in controversy shall be calculated on the basis of the average annual wage of employed persons in the previous year of the province, autonomous region, or municipality directly under the Central Government where the maritime court that actually accepts the case or its dispatched tribunal is located.

<a id="art-274"></a>第二百七十四条　人民法院受理小额诉讼案件，应当向当事人告知该类案件的审判组织、一审终审、审理期限、诉讼费用交纳标准等相关事项。

**Article 274** . When accepting a small-claim case, the People's Court shall inform the parties of relevant matters such as the adjudication organization for such cases, the first-instance judgment being final, the trial period, and the standards for paying litigation costs.

<a id="art-275"></a>第二百七十五条　小额诉讼案件的举证期限由人民法院确定，也可以由当事人协商一致并经人民法院准许，但一般不超过七日。  
被告要求书面答辩的，人民法院可以在征得其同意的基础上合理确定答辩期间，但最长不得超过十五日。  
当事人到庭后表示不需要举证期限和答辩期间的，人民法院可立即开庭审理。

**Article 275** . For a small-claim case, the time limit for producing evidence shall be determined by the People's Court, or may be agreed upon by both parties with the approval of the People's Court, but generally shall not exceed seven days.  
Where the defendant requests a written defense, the People's Court may reasonably determine the defense period based on obtaining the defendant's consent, but it shall not exceed fifteen days at the maximum.  
Where, after appearing in court, the parties state that they do not need a time limit for producing evidence or a defense period, the People's Court may hold the hearing immediately.

<a id="art-276"></a>第二百七十六条　当事人对小额诉讼案件提出管辖异议的，人民法院应当作出裁定。裁定一经作出即生效。

**Article 276** . Where a party raises a jurisdiction objection to a small-claim case, the People's Court shall issue a ruling. The ruling shall take effect once it is made.

<a id="art-277"></a>第二百七十七条　人民法院受理小额诉讼案件后，发现起诉不符合[民事诉讼法第一百二十二条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-122)规定的起诉条件的，裁定驳回起诉。裁定一经作出即生效。

**Article 277** . After accepting a small-claim case, if the People's Court discovers that the action does not meet the conditions for filing an action provided in Article 122 of the Civil Procedure Law, it shall rule to dismiss the action. The ruling shall take effect once it is made.

<a id="art-278"></a>第二百七十八条　因当事人申请增加或者变更诉讼请求、提出反诉、追加当事人等，致使案件不符合小额诉讼案件条件的，应当适用简易程序的其他规定审理。  
前款规定案件，应当适用普通程序审理的，裁定转为普通程序。  
适用简易程序的其他规定或者普通程序审理前，双方当事人已确认的事实，可以不再进行举证、质证。

**Article 278** . Where a case no longer meets the conditions for a small-claim case because a party applies to add or modify claims, file a counterclaim, or add parties, the case shall be tried under other provisions of the summary procedure.  
For a case provided in the preceding paragraph, if it shall be tried under the ordinary procedure, a ruling shall be made to convert it to the ordinary procedure.  
Before trial under other provisions of the summary procedure or under the ordinary procedure, facts already confirmed by both parties need not be subject to further adduction of evidence or cross-examination.

<a id="art-279"></a>第二百七十九条　当事人对按照小额诉讼案件审理有异议的，应当在开庭前提出。人民法院经审查，异议成立的，适用简易程序的其他规定审理或者裁定转为普通程序；异议不成立的，裁定驳回。裁定以口头方式作出的，应当记入笔录。

**Article 279** . Where a party objects to the trial of a case under the small-claim procedure, it shall raise the objection before the hearing. After examination by the People's Court, if the objection is established, the case shall be tried under other provisions of the summary procedure or a ruling shall be made to convert it to the ordinary procedure; if the objection is not established, it shall be ruled to be rejected. If the ruling is made orally, it shall be recorded in the record.

<a id="art-280"></a>第二百八十条　小额诉讼案件的裁判文书可以简化，主要记载当事人基本信息、诉讼请求、裁判主文等内容。

**Article 280** . The adjudication document of a small-claim case may be simplified, mainly recording the basic information of the parties, the claims, and the operative part of the adjudication.

<a id="art-281"></a>第二百八十一条　人民法院审理小额诉讼案件，本解释没有规定的，适用简易程序的其他规定。

**Article 281** . Where this Interpretation has no provisions on the trial of a small-claim case by the People's Court, the other provisions on the summary procedure shall apply.

### 十三、公益诉讼

<a id="art-282"></a>第二百八十二条　环境保护法、消费者权益保护法等法律规定的机关和有关组织对污染环境、侵害众多消费者合法权益等损害社会公共利益的行为，根据[民事诉讼法第五十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-58)规定提起公益诉讼，符合下列条件的，人民法院应当受理：  
(一)有明确的被告；  
(二)有具体的诉讼请求；  
(三)有社会公共利益受到损害的初步证据；  
(四)属于人民法院受理民事诉讼的范围和受诉人民法院管辖。

**Article 282** . Where organs and relevant organizations provided by laws such as the Environmental Protection Law and the Law on the Protection of Consumer Rights and Interests, in accordance with Article 58 of the Civil Procedure Law, file a public interest action against acts that damage social public interests, such as polluting the environment or infringing upon the lawful rights and interests of numerous consumers, the People's Court shall accept the action if the following conditions are met:  
(1) there is a definite defendant;  
(2) there are specific claims;  
(3) there is preliminary evidence that social public interests have been damaged;  
(4) the case falls within the scope of civil actions accepted by the People's Court and under the jurisdiction of the People's Court accepting the action.

<a id="art-283"></a>第二百八十三条　公益诉讼案件由侵权行为地或者被告住所地中级人民法院管辖，但法律、司法解释另有规定的除外。  
因污染海洋环境提起的公益诉讼，由污染发生地、损害结果地或者采取预防污染措施地海事法院管辖。  
对同一侵权行为分别向两个以上人民法院提起公益诉讼的，由最先立案的人民法院管辖，必要时由它们的共同上级人民法院指定管辖。

**Article 283** . Public interest cases shall be under the jurisdiction of the Intermediate People's Court of the place where the infringing act was committed or the place of domicile of the defendant, except as otherwise provided by law or judicial interpretation.  
A public interest action brought for pollution of the marine environment shall be under the jurisdiction of the maritime court of the place where the pollution occurred, where the damage resulted, or where pollution prevention measures were taken.  
Where public interest actions are brought against the same infringing act in two or more People's Courts, the People's Court that first docketed the case shall have jurisdiction; if necessary, their common higher-level People's Court shall designate jurisdiction.

<a id="art-284"></a>第二百八十四条　人民法院受理公益诉讼案件后，应当在十日内书面告知相关行政主管部门。

**Article 284** . After accepting a public interest case, the People's Court shall, within ten days, notify the relevant administrative department in charge in writing.

<a id="art-285"></a>第二百八十五条　人民法院受理公益诉讼案件后，依法可以提起诉讼的其他机关和有关组织，可以在开庭前向人民法院申请参加诉讼。人民法院准许参加诉讼的，列为共同原告。

**Article 285** . After the People's Court accepts a public interest case, other organs and relevant organizations that may bring an action according to law may apply to the People's Court to participate in the proceedings before the hearing. Where the People's Court permits their participation, they shall be listed as co-plaintiffs.

<a id="art-286"></a>第二百八十六条　人民法院受理公益诉讼案件，不影响同一侵权行为的受害人根据[民事诉讼法第一百二十二条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-122)规定提起诉讼。

**Article 286** . The acceptance of a public interest case by the People's Court shall not affect the right of victims of the same infringing act to bring an action in accordance with Article 122 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-287"></a>第二百八十七条　对公益诉讼案件，当事人可以和解，人民法院可以调解。  
当事人达成和解或者调解协议后，人民法院应当将和解或者调解协议进行公告。公告期间不得少于三十日。  
公告期满后，人民法院经审查，和解或者调解协议不违反社会公共利益的，应当出具调解书；和解或者调解协议违反社会公共利益的，不予出具调解书，继续对案件进行审理并依法作出裁判。

**Article 287** . For a public interest case, the parties may settle, and the People's Court may mediate.  
After the parties reach a settlement or mediation agreement, the People's Court shall make a public announcement of the settlement or mediation agreement. The announcement period shall not be less than thirty days.  
After the announcement period expires, if the People's Court, upon examination, finds that the settlement or mediation agreement does not violate social public interests, it shall issue a mediation statement; if the settlement or mediation agreement violates social public interests, it shall not issue a mediation statement and shall continue to hear the case and render a judgment or ruling according to law.

<a id="art-288"></a>第二百八十八条　公益诉讼案件的原告在法庭辩论终结后申请撤诉的，人民法院不予准许。

**Article 288** . Where the plaintiff in a public interest case applies to withdraw the action after the conclusion of court debate, the People's Court shall not permit it.

<a id="art-289"></a>第二百八十九条　公益诉讼案件的裁判发生法律效力后，其他依法具有原告资格的机关和有关组织就同一侵权行为另行提起公益诉讼的，人民法院裁定不予受理，但法律、司法解释另有规定的除外。

**Article 289** . After the adjudication of a public interest case takes legal effect, if other organs and relevant organizations that have plaintiff qualification according to law separately bring a public interest action against the same infringing act, the People's Court shall rule not to accept it, except as otherwise provided by law or judicial interpretation.

### 十四、第三人撤销之诉

<a id="art-290"></a>第二百九十条　第三人对已经发生法律效力的判决、裁定、调解书提起撤销之诉的，应当自知道或者应当知道其民事权益受到损害之日起六个月内，向作出生效判决、裁定、调解书的人民法院提出，并应当提供存在下列情形的证据材料：  
(一)因不能归责于本人的事由未参加诉讼；  
(二)发生法律效力的判决、裁定、调解书的全部或者部分内容错误；  
(三)发生法律效力的判决、裁定、调解书内容错误损害其民事权益。

**Article 290** . Where a third party initiates an action to set aside a legally effective judgment, ruling, or mediation statement, the third party shall file the action with the People's Court that rendered the effective judgment, ruling, or mediation statement within six months from the date on which the third party knew or should have known that its civil rights and interests were harmed, and shall provide evidentiary materials proving the existence of the following circumstances:  
(1) failure to participate in the proceedings for reasons not attributable to oneself;  
(2) errors in all or part of the contents of the legally effective judgment, ruling, or mediation statement;  
(3) errors in the contents of the legally effective judgment, ruling, or mediation statement harming its civil rights and interests.

<a id="art-291"></a>第二百九十一条　人民法院应当在收到起诉状和证据材料之日起五日内送交对方当事人，对方当事人可以自收到起诉状之日起十日内提出书面意见。  
人民法院应当对第三人提交的起诉状、证据材料以及对方当事人的书面意见进行审查。必要时，可以询问双方当事人。  
经审查，符合起诉条件的，人民法院应当在收到起诉状之日起三十日内立案。不符合起诉条件的，应当在收到起诉状之日起三十日内裁定不予受理。

**Article 291** . The People's Court shall deliver the complaint and evidentiary materials to the other party within five days from the date of receipt, and the other party may submit written opinions within ten days from the date of receipt of the complaint.  
The People's Court shall review the complaint, evidentiary materials submitted by the third party, and the written opinions of the other party. When necessary, it may question both parties.  
After review, if the conditions for filing an action are satisfied, the People's Court shall docket the case within thirty days from the date of receipt of the complaint. If the conditions are not satisfied, it shall rule not to accept the case within thirty days from the date of receipt of the complaint.

<a id="art-292"></a>第二百九十二条　人民法院对第三人撤销之诉案件，应当组成合议庭开庭审理。

**Article 292** . For a case involving a third party's action to set aside, the People's Court shall form a collegiate bench and hold a hearing in court.

<a id="art-293"></a>第二百九十三条　[民事诉讼法第五十九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-59)第三款规定的因不能归责于本人的事由未参加诉讼，是指没有被列为生效判决、裁定、调解书当事人，且无过错或者无明显过错的情形。包括：  
(一)不知道诉讼而未参加的；  
(二)申请参加未获准许的；  
(三)知道诉讼，但因客观原因无法参加的；  
(四)因其他不能归责于本人的事由未参加诉讼的。

**Article 293** . The failure to participate in the proceedings for reasons not attributable to oneself as provided for in Article 59, paragraph 3 of the Civil Procedure Law means a circumstance in which a person was not listed as a party to the effective judgment, ruling, or mediation statement and was not at fault or was not obviously at fault. It includes:  
(1) failing to participate because of unawareness of the litigation;  
(2) applying to participate but not being permitted;  
(3) being aware of the litigation but unable to participate due to objective reasons;  
(4) failing to participate for other reasons not attributable to oneself.

<a id="art-294"></a>第二百九十四条　[民事诉讼法第五十九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-59)第三款规定的判决、裁定、调解书的部分或者全部内容，是指判决、裁定的主文，调解书中处理当事人民事权利义务的结果。

**Article 294** . The partial or entire contents of a judgment, ruling, or mediation statement as provided for in Article 59, paragraph 3 of the Civil Procedure Law mean the operative part of the judgment or ruling and the results in the mediation statement that dispose of the parties' civil rights and obligations.

<a id="art-295"></a>第二百九十五条　对下列情形提起第三人撤销之诉的，人民法院不予受理：  
(一)适用特别程序、督促程序、公示催告程序、破产程序等非讼程序处理的案件；  
(二)婚姻无效、撤销或者解除婚姻关系等判决、裁定、调解书中涉及身份关系的内容；  
(三)[民事诉讼法第五十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-57)规定的未参加登记的权利人对代表人诉讼案件的生效裁判；  
(四)[民事诉讼法第五十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-58)规定的损害社会公共利益行为的受害人对公益诉讼案件的生效裁判。

**Article 295** . The People's Court shall not accept an action to set aside filed under any of the following circumstances:  
(1) cases handled under non-contentious procedures such as special procedures, supervision procedures, public summons procedures, or bankruptcy procedures;  
(2) contents involving personal status relations in judgments, rulings, or mediation statements such as those on nullity of marriage, revocation of marriage, or dissolution of marital relationship;  
(3) effective adjudications in representative action cases brought by rights holders who did not register to participate as provided for in Article 57 of the Civil Procedure Law;  
(4) effective adjudications in public interest litigation cases brought by victims of acts that harm social public interests as provided for in Article 58 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-296"></a>第二百九十六条　第三人提起撤销之诉，人民法院应当将该第三人列为原告，生效判决、裁定、调解书的当事人列为被告，但生效判决、裁定、调解书中没有承担责任的无独立请求权的第三人列为第三人。

**Article 296** . Where a third party initiates an action to set aside, the People's Court shall list the third party as the plaintiff, the parties to the effective judgment, ruling, or mediation statement as the defendants, and third parties without independent claims who did not bear liability in the effective judgment, ruling, or mediation statement as third parties.

<a id="art-297"></a>第二百九十七条　受理第三人撤销之诉案件后，原告提供相应担保，请求中止执行的，人民法院可以准许。

**Article 297** . After accepting a case of a third party's action to set aside, if the plaintiff provides corresponding security and requests suspension of enforcement, the People's Court may permit it.

<a id="art-298"></a>第二百九十八条　对第三人撤销或者部分撤销发生法律效力的判决、裁定、调解书内容的请求，人民法院经审理，按下列情形分别处理：  
(一)请求成立且确认其民事权利的主张全部或部分成立的，改变原判决、裁定、调解书内容的错误部分；  
(二)请求成立，但确认其全部或部分民事权利的主张不成立，或者未提出确认其民事权利请求的，撤销原判决、裁定、调解书内容的错误部分；  
(三)请求不成立的，驳回诉讼请求。  
对前款规定裁判不服的，当事人可以上诉。  
原判决、裁定、调解书的内容未改变或者未撤销的部分继续有效。

**Article 298** . With respect to a third party's request to set aside in whole or in part the contents of a legally effective judgment, ruling, or mediation statement, the People's Court shall, after trial, handle the matter separately according to the following circumstances:  
(1) If the request is established and the claims for confirmation of its civil rights are established in whole or in part, the erroneous parts of the original judgment, ruling, or mediation statement shall be modified;  
(2) If the request is established but the claims for confirmation of all or part of its civil rights are not established, or no claim for confirmation of its civil rights is raised, the erroneous parts of the original judgment, ruling, or mediation statement shall be set aside;  
(3) If the request is not established, the litigation claim shall be rejected.  
A party may appeal against an adjudication under the preceding paragraph.  
The parts of the original judgment, ruling, or mediation statement that are not modified or set aside shall continue to be effective.

<a id="art-299"></a>第二百九十九条　第三人撤销之诉案件审理期间，人民法院对生效判决、裁定、调解书裁定再审的，受理第三人撤销之诉的人民法院应当裁定将第三人的诉讼请求并入再审程序。但有证据证明原审当事人之间恶意串通损害第三人合法权益的，人民法院应当先行审理第三人撤销之诉案件，裁定中止再审诉讼。

**Article 299** . During the trial of a third party's action to set aside, if the People's Court rules to retry the effective judgment, ruling, or mediation statement, the People's Court that accepted the third party's action to set aside shall rule to consolidate the third party's litigation claims into the retrial procedure. However, if there is evidence proving that the parties in the original trial maliciously colluded to harm the third party's lawful rights and interests, the People's Court shall first try the third party's action to set aside and rule to suspend the retrial proceedings.

<a id="art-300"></a>第三百条　第三人诉讼请求并入再审程序审理的，按照下列情形分别处理：  
(一)按照第一审程序审理的，人民法院应当对第三人的诉讼请求一并审理，所作的判决可以上诉；  
(二)按照第二审程序审理的，人民法院可以调解，调解达不成协议的，应当裁定撤销原判决、裁定、调解书，发回一审法院重审，重审时应当列明第三人。

**Article 300** . Where a third party's litigation claims are consolidated into the retrial procedure for trial, they shall be handled separately according to the following circumstances:  
(1) If the case is tried under the first-instance procedure, the People's Court shall try the third party's litigation claims together, and the judgment rendered may be appealed;  
(2) If the case is tried under the second-instance procedure, the People's Court may mediate; if mediation fails to reach an agreement, the People's Court shall rule to set aside the original judgment, ruling, or mediation statement and remand the case to the first-instance court for retrial, during which the third party shall be listed.

<a id="art-301"></a>第三百零一条　第三人提起撤销之诉后，未中止生效判决、裁定、调解书执行的，执行法院对第三人依照[民事诉讼法第二百三十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-234)规定提出的执行异议，应予审查。第三人不服驳回执行异议裁定，申请对原判决、裁定、调解书再审的，人民法院不予受理。  
案外人对人民法院驳回其执行异议裁定不服，认为原判决、裁定、调解书内容错误损害其合法权益的，应当根据[民事诉讼法第二百三十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-234)规定申请再审，提起第三人撤销之诉的，人民法院不予受理。

**Article 301** . After a third party initiates an action to set aside, if the enforcement of the effective judgment, ruling, or mediation statement is not suspended, the enforcement court shall review the enforcement objection raised by the third party in accordance with Article 234 of the Civil Procedure Law. If the third party is dissatisfied with the ruling rejecting the enforcement objection and applies for retrial of the original judgment, ruling, or mediation statement, the People's Court shall not accept it.  
If a person other than a party is dissatisfied with the People's Court's ruling rejecting its enforcement objection and considers that the erroneous contents of the original judgment, ruling, or mediation statement harm its lawful rights and interests, it shall apply for retrial in accordance with Article 234 of the Civil Procedure Law; if it initiates a third party's action to set aside, the People's Court shall not accept it.

### 十五、执行异议之诉

<a id="art-302"></a>第三百零二条　根据[民事诉讼法第二百三十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-234)规定，案外人、当事人对执行异议裁定不服，自裁定送达之日起十五日内向人民法院提起执行异议之诉的，由执行法院管辖。

**Article 302** . In accordance with Article 234 of the Civil Procedure Law, where a person other than a party or a party is dissatisfied with a ruling on an enforcement objection and initiates an enforcement objection action before the People's Court within fifteen days from the date of service of the ruling, such action shall be under the jurisdiction of the enforcement court.

<a id="art-303"></a>第三百零三条　案外人提起执行异议之诉，除符合[民事诉讼法第一百二十二条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-122)规定外，还应当具备下列条件：  
(一)案外人的执行异议申请已经被人民法院裁定驳回；  
(二)有明确的排除对执行标的执行的诉讼请求，且诉讼请求与原判决、裁定无关；  
(三)自执行异议裁定送达之日起十五日内提起。  
人民法院应当在收到起诉状之日起十五日内决定是否立案。

**Article 303** . To initiate an enforcement objection action, a person other than a party shall, in addition to meeting the requirements of Article 122 of the Civil Procedure Law, satisfy the following conditions:  
(1) the application for enforcement objection by the person other than a party has been rejected by a ruling of the People's Court;  
(2) there is a clear litigation claim to exclude enforcement against the subject matter of enforcement, and the litigation claim is unrelated to the original judgment or ruling;  
(3) the action is initiated within fifteen days from the date of service of the ruling on the enforcement objection.  
The People's Court shall decide whether to docket the case within fifteen days from the date of receipt of the complaint.

<a id="art-304"></a>第三百零四条　申请执行人提起执行异议之诉，除符合[民事诉讼法第一百二十二条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-122)规定外，还应当具备下列条件：  
(一)依案外人执行异议申请，人民法院裁定中止执行；  
(二)有明确的对执行标的继续执行的诉讼请求，且诉讼请求与原判决、裁定无关；  
(三)自执行异议裁定送达之日起十五日内提起。  
人民法院应当在收到起诉状之日起十五日内决定是否立案。

**Article 304** . To initiate an enforcement objection action, the enforcement applicant shall, in addition to meeting the requirements of Article 122 of the Civil Procedure Law, satisfy the following conditions:  
(1) the People's Court has ruled to suspend enforcement upon the application for enforcement objection by the person other than a party;  
(2) there is a clear litigation claim to continue enforcement against the subject matter of enforcement, and the litigation claim is unrelated to the original judgment or ruling;  
(3) the action is initiated within fifteen days from the date of service of the ruling on the enforcement objection.  
The People's Court shall decide whether to docket the case within fifteen days from the date of receipt of the complaint.

<a id="art-305"></a>第三百零五条　案外人提起执行异议之诉的，以申请执行人为被告。被执行人反对案外人异议的，被执行人为共同被告；被执行人不反对案外人异议的，可以列被执行人为第三人。

**Article 305** . Where a person other than a party initiates an enforcement objection action, the enforcement applicant shall be listed as the defendant. If the person subject to enforcement opposes the objection of the person other than a party, the person subject to enforcement shall be a co-defendant; if the person subject to enforcement does not oppose the objection, the person subject to enforcement may be listed as a third party.

<a id="art-306"></a>第三百零六条　申请执行人提起执行异议之诉的，以案外人为被告。被执行人反对申请执行人主张的，以案外人和被执行人为共同被告；被执行人不反对申请执行人主张的，可以列被执行人为第三人。

**Article 306** . Where the enforcement applicant initiates an enforcement objection action, the person other than a party shall be listed as the defendant. If the person subject to enforcement opposes the claims of the enforcement applicant, the person other than a party and the person subject to enforcement shall be co-defendants; if the person subject to enforcement does not oppose the claims of the enforcement applicant, the person subject to enforcement may be listed as a third party.

<a id="art-307"></a>第三百零七条　申请执行人对中止执行裁定未提起执行异议之诉，被执行人提起执行异议之诉的，人民法院告知其另行起诉。

**Article 307** . Where the enforcement applicant does not initiate an enforcement objection action against the ruling suspending enforcement, and the person subject to enforcement initiates an enforcement objection action, the People's Court shall inform the latter to file a separate action.

<a id="art-308"></a>第三百零八条　人民法院审理执行异议之诉案件，适用普通程序。

**Article 308** . The People's Court shall apply the ordinary procedure in trying cases of enforcement objection actions.

<a id="art-309"></a>第三百零九条　案外人或者申请执行人提起执行异议之诉的，案外人应当就其对执行标的享有足以排除强制执行的民事权益承担举证证明责任。

**Article 309** . Where a person other than a party or the enforcement applicant initiates an enforcement objection action, the person other than a party shall bear the burden of proof to prove that it has civil rights and interests in the subject matter of enforcement sufficient to exclude compulsory enforcement.

<a id="art-310"></a>第三百一十条　对案外人提起的执行异议之诉，人民法院经审理，按照下列情形分别处理：  
(一)案外人就执行标的享有足以排除强制执行的民事权益的，判决不得执行该执行标的；  
(二)案外人就执行标的不享有足以排除强制执行的民事权益的，判决驳回诉讼请求。  
案外人同时提出确认其权利的诉讼请求的，人民法院可以在判决中一并作出裁判。

**Article 310** . With respect to an enforcement objection action initiated by a person other than a party, the People's Court shall, after trial, handle the matter separately according to the following circumstances:  
(1) If the person other than a party has civil rights and interests in the subject matter of enforcement sufficient to exclude compulsory enforcement, it shall be adjudicated that the subject matter of enforcement shall not be enforced;  
(2) If the person other than a party does not have civil rights and interests in the subject matter of enforcement sufficient to exclude compulsory enforcement, the litigation claim shall be rejected.  
If the person other than a party also raises a litigation claim to confirm its rights, the People's Court may make an adjudication on such claim together in the judgment.

<a id="art-311"></a>第三百一十一条　对申请执行人提起的执行异议之诉，人民法院经审理，按照下列情形分别处理：  
(一)案外人就执行标的不享有足以排除强制执行的民事权益的，判决准许执行该执行标的；  
(二)案外人就执行标的享有足以排除强制执行的民事权益的，判决驳回诉讼请求。

**Article 311** . With respect to an enforcement objection action initiated by the enforcement applicant, the People's Court shall, after trial, handle the matter separately according to the following circumstances:  
(1) If the person other than a party does not have civil rights and interests in the subject matter of enforcement sufficient to exclude compulsory enforcement, it shall be adjudicated that enforcement of the subject matter of enforcement is permitted;  
(2) If the person other than a party has civil rights and interests in the subject matter of enforcement sufficient to exclude compulsory enforcement, the litigation claim shall be rejected.

<a id="art-312"></a>第三百一十二条　对案外人执行异议之诉，人民法院判决不得对执行标的执行的，执行异议裁定失效。  
对申请执行人执行异议之诉，人民法院判决准许对该执行标的执行的，执行异议裁定失效，执行法院可以根据申请执行人的申请或者依职权恢复执行。

**Article 312** . Where, in an enforcement objection action brought by a person other than a party, the People's Court adjudicates that the subject matter of enforcement shall not be enforced, the ruling on the enforcement objection shall cease to have effect.  
Where, in an enforcement objection action brought by the enforcement applicant, the People's Court adjudicates that enforcement of the subject matter of enforcement is permitted, the ruling on the enforcement objection shall cease to have effect, and the enforcement court may resume enforcement upon the application of the enforcement applicant or ex officio.

<a id="art-313"></a>第三百一十三条　案外人执行异议之诉审理期间，人民法院不得对执行标的进行处分。申请执行人请求人民法院继续执行并提供相应担保的，人民法院可以准许。  
被执行人与案外人恶意串通，通过执行异议、执行异议之诉妨害执行的，人民法院应当依照[民事诉讼法第一百一十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-116)规定处理。申请执行人因此受到损害的，可以提起诉讼要求被执行人、案外人赔偿。

**Article 313** . During the trial of an enforcement objection action brought by a person other than a party, the People's Court shall not dispose of the subject matter of enforcement. If the enforcement applicant requests the People's Court to continue enforcement and provides corresponding security, the People's Court may permit it.  
If the person subject to enforcement and the person other than a party maliciously collude to obstruct enforcement through enforcement objections or enforcement objection actions, the People's Court shall handle the matter in accordance with Article 116 of the Civil Procedure Law. If the enforcement applicant suffers damage as a result, it may initiate an action to claim compensation from the person subject to enforcement and the person other than a party.

<a id="art-314"></a>第三百一十四条　人民法院对执行标的裁定中止执行后，申请执行人在法律规定的期间内未提起执行异议之诉的，人民法院应当自起诉期限届满之日起七日内解除对该执行标的采取的执行措施。

**Article 314** . After the People's Court rules to suspend enforcement of the subject matter of enforcement, if the enforcement applicant does not initiate an enforcement objection action within the period prescribed by law, the People's Court shall, within seven days from the date of expiration of the period for filing an action, lift the enforcement measures taken against the subject matter of enforcement.

### 十六、第二审程序

<a id="art-315"></a>第三百一十五条　双方当事人和第三人都提起上诉的，均列为上诉人。人民法院可以依职权确定第二审程序中当事人的诉讼地位。

**Article 315** . Where both parties and the third party all file appeals, they shall all be listed as appellants. The People's Court may, ex officio, determine the litigation status of the parties in the second-instance procedure.

<a id="art-316"></a>第三百一十六条　[民事诉讼法第一百七十三条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-173)、第一百七十四条规定的对方当事人包括被上诉人和原审其他当事人。

**Article 316** . The other parties as provided for in Articles 173 and 174 of the Civil Procedure Law include the respondent and other parties in the original trial.

<a id="art-317"></a>第三百一十七条　必要共同诉讼人的一人或者部分人提起上诉的，按下列情形分别处理：  
(一)上诉仅对与对方当事人之间权利义务分担有意见，不涉及其他共同诉讼人利益的，对方当事人为被上诉人，未上诉的同一方当事人依原审诉讼地位列明；  
(二)上诉仅对共同诉讼人之间权利义务分担有意见，不涉及对方当事人利益的，未上诉的同一方当事人为被上诉人，对方当事人依原审诉讼地位列明；  
(三)上诉对双方当事人之间以及共同诉讼人之间权利义务承担有意见的，未提起上诉的其他当事人均为被上诉人。

**Article 317** . Where one or some of the necessary co-litigants file an appeal, the matter shall be handled separately according to the following circumstances:  
(1) If the appeal only objects to the allocation of rights and obligations with the other party and does not involve the interests of other co-litigants, the other party shall be the respondent, and the non-appealing parties on the same side shall be listed according to their litigation status in the original trial;  
(2) If the appeal only objects to the allocation of rights and obligations among the co-litigants and does not involve the interests of the other party, the non-appealing parties on the same side shall be the respondents, and the other party shall be listed according to its litigation status in the original trial;  
(3) If the appeal objects to the assumption of rights and obligations between the parties and among the co-litigants, all other parties who did not file an appeal shall be respondents.

<a id="art-318"></a>第三百一十八条　一审宣判时或者判决书、裁定书送达时，当事人口头表示上诉的，人民法院应告知其必须在法定上诉期间内递交上诉状。未在法定上诉期间内递交上诉状的，视为未提起上诉。虽递交上诉状，但未在指定的期限内交纳上诉费的，按自动撤回上诉处理。

**Article 318** . Where a party makes an oral statement of appeal at the time of pronouncement of the first-instance judgment or at the time of service of the judgment or ruling, the People's Court shall inform the party that it must submit a written appeal within the statutory appeal period. If no written appeal is submitted within the statutory appeal period, the appeal shall be deemed not to have been filed. If a written appeal is submitted but the appeal fee is not paid within the specified period, the appeal shall be treated as automatically withdrawn.

<a id="art-319"></a>第三百一十九条　无民事行为能力人、限制民事行为能力人的法定代理人，可以代理当事人提起上诉。

**Article 319** . The statutory agent of a person without capacity for civil conduct or a person with limited capacity for civil conduct may file an appeal on behalf of the party.

<a id="art-320"></a>第三百二十条　上诉案件的当事人死亡或者终止的，人民法院依法通知其权利义务承继者参加诉讼。  
需要终结诉讼的，适用[民事诉讼法第一百五十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-154)规定。

**Article 320** . Where a party in an appeal case dies or is terminated, the People's Court shall, according to law, notify the successor to its rights and obligations to participate in the proceedings.  
Where the litigation needs to be concluded, the provisions of Article 154 of the Civil Procedure Law shall apply.

<a id="art-321"></a>第三百二十一条　第二审人民法院应当围绕当事人的上诉请求进行审理。  
当事人没有提出请求的，不予审理，但一审判决违反法律禁止性规定，或者损害国家利益、社会公共利益、他人合法权益的除外。

**Article 321** . The People's Court of second instance shall conduct the trial centering on the parties' claims on appeal.  
Where a party does not raise a claim, it shall not be tried, except where the first-instance judgment violates prohibitive provisions of law, or harms national interests, social public interests, or the lawful rights and interests of others.

<a id="art-322"></a>第三百二十二条　开庭审理的上诉案件，第二审人民法院可以依照[民事诉讼法第一百三十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-136)第四项规定进行审理前的准备。

**Article 322** . For an appeal case tried in court, the People's Court of second instance may make preparations before trial in accordance with item (4) of Article 136 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-323"></a>第三百二十三条　下列情形，可以认定为[民事诉讼法第一百七十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-177)第一款第四项规定的严重违反法定程序：  
(一)审判组织的组成不合法的；  
(二)应当回避的审判人员未回避的；  
(三)无诉讼行为能力人未经法定代理人代为诉讼的；  
(四)违法剥夺当事人辩论权利的。

**Article 323** . The following circumstances may be found to constitute serious violation of statutory procedures as provided for in item (4) of paragraph 1 of Article 177 of the Civil Procedure Law:  
(1) the composition of the adjudication organization is unlawful;  
(2) a judicial officer who should have withdrawn failed to withdraw;  
(3) a person without capacity for litigation did not have a statutory agent to act on his or her behalf in the litigation;  
(4) the parties' right to debate was unlawfully deprived.

<a id="art-324"></a>第三百二十四条　对当事人在第一审程序中已经提出的诉讼请求，原审人民法院未作审理、判决的，第二审人民法院可以根据当事人自愿的原则进行调解；调解不成的，发回重审。

**Article 324** . Where the original-instance People's Court failed to try or adjudicate a litigation claim already raised by a party in the first-instance procedure, the People's Court of second instance may mediate in accordance with the principle of voluntariness of the parties; if mediation fails, the case shall be remanded for retrial.

<a id="art-325"></a>第三百二十五条　必须参加诉讼的当事人或者有独立请求权的第三人，在第一审程序中未参加诉讼，第二审人民法院可以根据当事人自愿的原则予以调解；调解不成的，发回重审。

**Article 325** . Where a party that must participate in the litigation or a third party with independent claims did not participate in the litigation in the first-instance procedure, the People's Court of second instance may mediate in accordance with the principle of voluntariness of the parties; if mediation fails, the case shall be remanded for retrial.

<a id="art-326"></a>第三百二十六条　在第二审程序中，原审原告增加独立的诉讼请求或者原审被告提出反诉的，第二审人民法院可以根据当事人自愿的原则就新增加的诉讼请求或者反诉进行调解；调解不成的，告知当事人另行起诉。  
双方当事人同意由第二审人民法院一并审理的，第二审人民法院可以一并裁判。

**Article 326** . In the second-instance procedure, if the original plaintiff adds an independent litigation claim or the original defendant files a counterclaim, the People's Court of second instance may, in accordance with the principle of voluntariness of the parties, mediate on the newly added litigation claim or the counterclaim; if mediation fails, the parties shall be informed to file a separate action.  
If both parties agree to have the People's Court of second instance try the matter together, the People's Court of second instance may adjudicate together.

<a id="art-327"></a>第三百二十七条　一审判决不准离婚的案件，上诉后，第二审人民法院认为应当判决离婚的，可以根据当事人自愿的原则，与子女抚养、财产问题一并调解；调解不成的，发回重审。  
双方当事人同意由第二审人民法院一并审理的，第二审人民法院可以一并裁判。

**Article 327** . For a case in which the first-instance judgment did not grant divorce, if, after appeal, the People's Court of second instance considers that divorce should be adjudicated, it may, in accordance with the principle of voluntariness of the parties, mediate together with the issues of child support and property; if mediation fails, the case shall be remanded for retrial.  
If both parties agree to have the People's Court of second instance try the matter together, the People's Court of second instance may adjudicate together.

<a id="art-328"></a>第三百二十八条　人民法院依照第二审程序审理案件，认为依法不应由人民法院受理的，可以由第二审人民法院直接裁定撤销原裁判，驳回起诉。

**Article 328** . Where the People's Court tries a case under the second-instance procedure and considers that, according to law, the case should not have been accepted by the People's Court, the People's Court of second instance may directly rule to set aside the original adjudication and dismiss the action.

<a id="art-329"></a>第三百二十九条　人民法院依照第二审程序审理案件，认为第一审人民法院受理案件违反专属管辖规定的，应当裁定撤销原裁判并移送有管辖权的人民法院。

**Article 329** . Where the People's Court tries a case under the second-instance procedure and considers that the acceptance of the case by the first-instance People's Court violated the provisions on exclusive jurisdiction, it shall rule to set aside the original adjudication and transfer the case to the People's Court with jurisdiction.

<a id="art-330"></a>第三百三十条　第二审人民法院查明第一审人民法院作出的不予受理裁定有错误的，应当在撤销原裁定的同时，指令第一审人民法院立案受理；查明第一审人民法院作出的驳回起诉裁定有错误的，应当在撤销原裁定的同时，指令第一审人民法院审理。

**Article 330** . Where the People's Court of second instance finds that the ruling of non-acceptance made by the first-instance People's Court was erroneous, it shall, while setting aside the original ruling, instruct the first-instance People's Court to docket and accept the case; where it finds that the ruling dismissing the action made by the first-instance People's Court was erroneous, it shall, while setting aside the original ruling, instruct the first-instance People's Court to try the case.

<a id="art-331"></a>第三百三十一条　第二审人民法院对下列上诉案件，依照[民事诉讼法第一百七十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-176)规定可以不开庭审理：  
(一)不服不予受理、管辖权异议和驳回起诉裁定的；  
(二)当事人提出的上诉请求明显不能成立的；  
(三)原判决、裁定认定事实清楚，但适用法律错误的；  
(四)原判决严重违反法定程序，需要发回重审的。

**Article 331** . The People's Court of second instance may, in accordance with Article 176 of the Civil Procedure Law, try the following appeal cases without a hearing:  
(1) appeals against rulings on non-acceptance, jurisdictional objection, and dismissal of action;  
(2) cases where the appellant's claims on appeal are clearly untenable;  
(3) cases where the facts found in the original judgment or ruling are clear but the law was incorrectly applied;  
(4) cases where the original judgment seriously violates statutory procedures and needs to be remanded for retrial.

<a id="art-332"></a>第三百三十二条　原判决、裁定认定事实或者适用法律虽有瑕疵，但裁判结果正确的，第二审人民法院可以在判决、裁定中纠正瑕疵后，依照[民事诉讼法第一百七十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-177)第一款第一项规定予以维持。

**Article 332** . Where the original judgment or ruling has defects in fact finding or application of law, but the result of the adjudication is correct, the People's Court of second instance may, after correcting the defects in the judgment or ruling, affirm the original adjudication in accordance with item (1) of paragraph 1 of Article 177 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-333"></a>第三百三十三条　[民事诉讼法第一百七十七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-177)第一款第三项规定的基本事实，是指用以确定当事人主体资格、案件性质、民事权利义务等对原判决、裁定的结果有实质性影响的事实。

**Article 333** . The basic facts as provided for in item (3) of paragraph 1 of Article 177 of the Civil Procedure Law mean facts used to determine the parties' qualifications, the nature of the case, civil rights and obligations, and other facts that have a substantive impact on the result of the original judgment or ruling.

<a id="art-334"></a>第三百三十四条　在第二审程序中，作为当事人的法人或者其他组织分立的，人民法院可以直接将分立后的法人或者其他组织列为共同诉讼人；合并的，将合并后的法人或者其他组织列为当事人。

**Article 334** . In the second-instance procedure, where a legal person or other organization that is a party is divided, the People's Court may directly list the divided legal persons or other organizations as co-litigants; where it is merged, the merged legal person or other organization shall be listed as a party.

<a id="art-335"></a>第三百三十五条　在第二审程序中，当事人申请撤回上诉，人民法院经审查认为一审判决确有错误，或者当事人之间恶意串通损害国家利益、社会公共利益、他人合法权益的，不应准许。

**Article 335** . In the second-instance procedure, where a party applies to withdraw the appeal, the People's Court shall not permit the withdrawal if, after review, it considers that the first-instance judgment is indeed erroneous, or the parties maliciously colluded to harm national interests, social public interests, or the lawful rights and interests of others.

<a id="art-336"></a>第三百三十六条　在第二审程序中，原审原告申请撤回起诉，经其他当事人同意，且不损害国家利益、社会公共利益、他人合法权益的，人民法院可以准许。准许撤诉的，应当一并裁定撤销一审裁判。  
原审原告在第二审程序中撤回起诉后重复起诉的，人民法院不予受理。

**Article 336** . In the second-instance procedure, if the original plaintiff applies to withdraw the action, with the consent of the other parties, and if it does not harm national interests, social public interests, or the lawful rights and interests of others, the People's Court may permit it. Where withdrawal of the action is permitted, the first-instance adjudication shall be simultaneously ruled to be set aside.  
If the original plaintiff files a repeated action after withdrawing the action in the second-instance procedure, the People's Court shall not accept it.

<a id="art-337"></a>第三百三十七条　当事人在第二审程序中达成和解协议的，人民法院可以根据当事人的请求，对双方达成的和解协议进行审查并制作调解书送达当事人；因和解而申请撤诉，经审查符合撤诉条件的，人民法院应予准许。

**Article 337** . Where the parties reach a settlement agreement in the second-instance procedure, the People's Court may, upon the request of the parties, review the settlement agreement reached by both parties and produce a mediation statement to be served on the parties; where a party applies for withdrawal of the action due to settlement, upon review that the conditions for withdrawal are met, the People's Court shall permit it.

<a id="art-338"></a>第三百三十八条　第二审人民法院宣告判决可以自行宣判，也可以委托原审人民法院或者当事人所在地人民法院代行宣判。

**Article 338** . The People's Court of second instance may pronounce the judgment by itself, or may entrust the original-instance People's Court or the People's Court at the place where the party is located to pronounce the judgment on its behalf.

<a id="art-339"></a>第三百三十九条　人民法院审理对裁定的上诉案件，应当在第二审立案之日起三十日内作出终审裁定。有特殊情况需要延长审限的，由本院院长批准。

**Article 339** . The People's Court shall, for an appeal case against a ruling, render a final ruling within thirty days from the date of docketing in the second instance. If there are special circumstances requiring an extension of the trial period, the approval of the president of the court shall be obtained.

<a id="art-340"></a>第三百四十条　当事人在第一审程序中实施的诉讼行为，在第二审程序中对该当事人仍具有拘束力。  
当事人推翻其在第一审程序中实施的诉讼行为时，人民法院应当责令其说明理由。理由不成立的，不予支持。

**Article 340** . Litigation acts performed by a party in the first-instance procedure shall still be binding on that party in the second-instance procedure.  
Where a party repudiates its litigation acts performed in the first-instance procedure, the People's Court shall order it to explain the reasons. If the reasons are not established, they shall not be supported.

### 十七、特别程序

<a id="art-341"></a>第三百四十一条　宣告失踪或者宣告死亡案件，人民法院可以根据申请人的请求，清理下落不明人的财产，并指定案件审理期间的财产管理人。公告期满后，人民法院判决宣告失踪的，应当同时依照[民法典第四十二条](../../民事与商事/民法典/中华人民共和国民法典.md#art-42)的规定指定失踪人的财产代管人。

**Article 341** . In a case for declaration of missing person or declaration of death, the People's Court may, upon the request of the applicant, inventory the property of the person whose whereabouts are unknown, and appoint a property manager for the period of trial. After the expiration of the public announcement period, if the People's Court adjudicates to declare the person missing, it shall, at the same time, appoint a property custodian for the missing person in accordance with Article 42 of the Civil Code.

<a id="art-342"></a>第三百四十二条　失踪人的财产代管人经人民法院指定后，代管人申请变更代管的，比照民事诉讼法特别程序的有关规定进行审理。申请理由成立的，裁定撤销申请人的代管人身份，同时另行指定财产代管人；申请理由不成立的，裁定驳回申请。  
失踪人的其他利害关系人申请变更代管的，人民法院应当告知其以原指定的代管人为被告起诉，并按普通程序进行审理。

**Article 342** . Where a property custodian for a missing person has been designated by the People's Court, and the custodian applies to change the custodianship, the case shall be tried by reference to the relevant provisions on special procedures in the Civil Procedure Law. If the reasons for the application are established, the ruling shall revoke the applicant's status as custodian and designate another property custodian at the same time; if the reasons are not established, the application shall be rejected by ruling.  
Where other interested persons of the missing person apply to change the custodianship, the People's Court shall inform them to sue with the originally designated custodian as the defendant, and the case shall be tried under the ordinary procedure.

<a id="art-343"></a>第三百四十三条　人民法院判决宣告公民失踪后，利害关系人向人民法院申请宣告失踪人死亡，自失踪之日起满四年的，人民法院应当受理，宣告失踪的判决即是该公民失踪的证明，审理中仍应依照[民事诉讼法第一百九十二条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-192)规定进行公告。

**Article 343** . After the People's Court adjudicates to declare a citizen missing, if an interested person applies to the People's Court for declaration of death of the missing person, and four years have elapsed since the date of disappearance, the People's Court shall accept the application. The judgment declaring the person missing shall serve as proof of the citizen's disappearance, and during the trial, a public announcement shall still be made in accordance with Article 192 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-344"></a>第三百四十四条　符合法律规定的多个利害关系人提出宣告失踪、宣告死亡申请的，列为共同申请人。

**Article 344** . Where multiple interested persons who meet the legal requirements file applications for declaration of missing person or declaration of death, they shall be listed as co-applicants.

<a id="art-345"></a>第三百四十五条　寻找下落不明人的公告应当记载下列内容：  
(一)被申请人应当在规定期间内向受理法院申报其具体地址及其联系方式。否则，被申请人将被宣告失踪、宣告死亡；  
(二)凡知悉被申请人生存现状的人，应当在公告期间内将其所知道情况向受理法院报告。

**Article 345** . The public announcement for seeking the whereabouts of a missing person shall record the following contents:  
(1) The respondent shall, within the specified period, report his or her specific address and contact information to the accepting court. Otherwise, the respondent shall be declared missing or declared dead;  
(2) Any person who knows the current living status of the respondent shall, within the public announcement period, report what he or she knows to the accepting court.

<a id="art-346"></a>第三百四十六条　人民法院受理宣告失踪、宣告死亡案件后，作出判决前，申请人撤回申请的，人民法院应当裁定终结案件，但其他符合法律规定的利害关系人加入程序要求继续审理的除外。

**Article 346** . After the People's Court accepts a case for declaration of disappearance or declaration of death, if the applicant withdraws the application before a judgment is rendered, the People's Court shall rule to terminate the case, except where other interested parties who meet the legal requirements join the proceedings and request continued adjudication.

<a id="art-347"></a>第三百四十七条　在诉讼中，当事人的利害关系人或者有关组织提出该当事人不能辨认或者不能完全辨认自己的行为，要求宣告该当事人无民事行为能力或者限制民事行为能力的，应由利害关系人或者有关组织向人民法院提出申请，由受诉人民法院按照特别程序立案审理，原诉讼中止。

**Article 347** . In the course of litigation, if an interested party or relevant organization of a party asserts that the party cannot recognize or cannot fully recognize his or her own conduct, and requests that the party be declared a person without capacity for civil conduct or with limited capacity for civil conduct, the interested party or relevant organization shall file an application with the People's Court, and the People's Court accepting the case shall docket and adjudicate the case under the special procedure, and the original litigation shall be suspended.

<a id="art-348"></a>第三百四十八条　认定财产无主案件，公告期间有人对财产提出请求的，人民法院应当裁定终结特别程序，告知申请人另行起诉，适用普通程序审理。

**Article 348** . In a case for determining that property is ownerless, if a person makes a claim to the property during the public notice period, the People's Court shall rule to terminate the special procedure, inform the applicant to file a separate action, and the case shall be adjudicated under the ordinary procedure.

<a id="art-349"></a>第三百四十九条　被指定的监护人不服居民委员会、村民委员会或者民政部门指定，应当自接到通知之日起三十日内向人民法院提出异议。经审理，认为指定并无不当的，裁定驳回异议；指定不当的，判决撤销指定，同时另行指定监护人。判决书应当送达异议人、原指定单位及判决指定的监护人。  
有关当事人依照[民法典第三十一条](../../民事与商事/民法典/中华人民共和国民法典.md#art-31)第一款规定直接向人民法院申请指定监护人的，适用特别程序审理，判决指定监护人。判决书应当送达申请人、判决指定的监护人。

**Article 349** . If the designated guardian refuses to accept the designation by the residents' committee, villagers' committee, or civil affairs department, he or she shall raise an objection with the People's Court within 30 days from receipt of the notice. Upon adjudication, if the designation is found to be appropriate, the objection shall be rejected by ruling; if the designation is inappropriate, the designation shall be revoked by judgment, and a guardian shall be separately designated at the same time. The judgment shall be served on the objector, the original designating entity, and the guardian designated by the judgment.  
Where a relevant party directly applies to the People's Court for designation of a guardian in accordance with paragraph 1 of Article 31 of the Civil Code, the special procedure shall apply for adjudication, and a guardian shall be designated by judgment. The judgment shall be served on the applicant and the guardian designated by the judgment.

<a id="art-350"></a>第三百五十条　申请认定公民无民事行为能力或者限制民事行为能力的案件，被申请人没有近亲属的，人民法院可以指定经被申请人住所地的居民委员会、村民委员会或者民政部门同意，且愿意担任代理人的个人或者组织为代理人。  
没有前款规定的代理人的，由被申请人住所地的居民委员会、村民委员会或者民政部门担任代理人。  
代理人可以是一人，也可以是同一顺序中的两人。

**Article 350** . In a case applying for determination that a citizen has no capacity for civil conduct or has limited capacity for civil conduct, if the respondent has no close relatives, the People's Court may designate, as the agent, an individual or organization that has obtained the consent of the residents' committee, villagers' committee, or civil affairs department at the respondent's domicile and is willing to serve as the agent.  
If there is no agent as provided in the preceding paragraph, the residents' committee, villagers' committee, or civil affairs department at the respondent's domicile shall serve as the agent.  
The agent may be one person, or two persons of the same order.

<a id="art-351"></a>第三百五十一条　申请司法确认调解协议的，双方当事人应当本人或者由符合[民事诉讼法第六十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-61)规定的代理人依照[民事诉讼法第二百零一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-201)的规定提出申请。

**Article 351** . To apply for judicial confirmation of a mediation agreement, both parties shall themselves, or through an agent who meets the requirements of Article 61 of the Civil Procedure Law, submit the application in accordance with Article 201 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-352"></a>第三百五十二条　调解组织自行开展的调解，有两个以上调解组织参与的，符合[民事诉讼法第二百零一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-201)规定的各调解组织所在地人民法院均有管辖权。  
双方当事人可以共同向符合[民事诉讼法第二百零一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-201)规定的其中一个有管辖权的人民法院提出申请；双方当事人共同向两个以上有管辖权的人民法院提出申请的，由最先立案的人民法院管辖。

**Article 352** . Where a mediation organization independently conducts mediation, and more than two mediation organizations participate, the People's Courts at the locations of each mediation organization that meet the requirements of Article 201 of the Civil Procedure Law shall all have jurisdiction.  
Both parties may jointly file an application with one of the People's Courts that has jurisdiction and meets the requirements of Article 201 of the Civil Procedure Law; where both parties jointly file applications with two or more People's Courts that have jurisdiction, the People's Court that first dockets the case shall have jurisdiction.

<a id="art-353"></a>第三百五十三条　当事人申请司法确认调解协议，可以采用书面形式或者口头形式。当事人口头申请的，人民法院应当记入笔录，并由当事人签名、捺印或者盖章。

**Article 353** . A party applying for judicial confirmation of a mediation agreement may do so in written or oral form. Where a party makes an oral application, the People's Court shall record it in the transcripts, and the party shall sign, fingerprint, or affix a seal.

<a id="art-354"></a>第三百五十四条　当事人申请司法确认调解协议，应当向人民法院提交调解协议、调解组织主持调解的证明，以及与调解协议相关的财产权利证明等材料，并提供双方当事人的身份、住所、联系方式等基本信息。  
当事人未提交上述材料的，人民法院应当要求当事人限期补交。

**Article 354** . A party applying for judicial confirmation of a mediation agreement shall submit to the People's Court the mediation agreement, proof that the mediation organization presided over the mediation, and materials such as proof of property rights related to the mediation agreement, and shall provide basic information such as the identities, domiciles, and contact information of both parties.  
Where a party fails to submit the above materials, the People's Court shall require the party to supplement them within a specified time limit.

<a id="art-355"></a>第三百五十五条　当事人申请司法确认调解协议，有下列情形之一的，人民法院裁定不予受理：  
(一)不属于人民法院受理范围的；  
(二)不属于收到申请的人民法院管辖的；  
(三)申请确认婚姻关系、亲子关系、收养关系等身份关系无效、有效或者解除的；  
(四)涉及适用其他特别程序、公示催告程序、破产程序审理的；  
(五)调解协议内容涉及物权、知识产权确权的。  
人民法院受理申请后，发现有上述不予受理情形的，应当裁定驳回当事人的申请。

**Article 355** . Where a party applies for judicial confirmation of a mediation agreement, in any of the following circumstances, the People's Court shall rule not to accept the application:  
(1) The matter is not within the scope of cases accepted by the People's Court;  
(2) The matter is not within the jurisdiction of the People's Court that received the application;  
(3) The application is for confirmation of the invalidity, validity, or dissolution of status relationships such as marital relationship, parent-child relationship, or adoption relationship;  
(4) The matter involves adjudication under other special procedures, proceedings for public summons, or bankruptcy proceedings;  
(5) The content of the mediation agreement involves confirmation of rights in rem or intellectual property rights.  
After accepting the application, if the People's Court discovers any of the above circumstances for non-acceptance, it shall rule to reject the party's application.

<a id="art-356"></a>第三百五十六条　人民法院审查相关情况时，应当通知双方当事人共同到场对案件进行核实。  
人民法院经审查，认为当事人的陈述或者提供的证明材料不充分、不完备或者有疑义的，可以要求当事人限期补充陈述或者补充证明材料。必要时，人民法院可以向调解组织核实有关情况。

**Article 356** . When the People's Court examines the relevant circumstances, it shall notify both parties to appear together to verify the case.  
Where the People's Court, upon examination, considers that the parties' statements or the evidentiary materials provided are insufficient, incomplete, or doubtful, it may require the parties to supplement statements or evidence within a specified time limit. If necessary, the People's Court may verify the relevant circumstances with the mediation organization.

<a id="art-357"></a>第三百五十七条　确认调解协议的裁定作出前，当事人撤回申请的，人民法院可以裁定准许。  
当事人无正当理由未在限期内补充陈述、补充证明材料或者拒不接受询问的，人民法院可以按撤回申请处理。

**Article 357** . Before a ruling confirming a mediation agreement is made, if a party withdraws the application, the People's Court may rule to permit the withdrawal.  
Where a party, without justifiable reasons, fails to supplement statements or evidentiary materials within the time limit, or refuses to accept inquiry, the People's Court may treat the application as withdrawn.

<a id="art-358"></a>第三百五十八条　经审查，调解协议有下列情形之一的，人民法院应当裁定驳回申请：  
(一)违反法律强制性规定的；  
(二)损害国家利益、社会公共利益、他人合法权益的；  
(三)违背公序良俗的；  
(四)违反自愿原则的；  
(五)内容不明确的；  
(六)其他不能进行司法确认的情形。

**Article 358** . Upon examination, if the mediation agreement has any of the following circumstances, the People's Court shall rule to reject the application:  
(1) It violates mandatory provisions of the law;  
(2) It harms national interests, public interests, or the lawful rights and interests of others;  
(3) It violates public order and good morals;  
(4) It violates the principle of voluntariness;  
(5) Its content is unclear;  
(6) Other circumstances where judicial confirmation cannot be made.

<a id="art-359"></a>第三百五十九条　[民事诉讼法第二百零三条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-203)规定的担保物权人，包括抵押权人、质权人、留置权人；其他有权请求实现担保物权的人，包括抵押人、出质人、财产被留置的债务人或者所有权人等。

**Article 359** . The security interest holders provided for in Article 203 of the Civil Procedure Law include mortgagees, pledgees, and lienholders; other persons entitled to request realization of security interest include mortgagors, pledgors, debtors whose property is subject to a lien, or owners, etc.

<a id="art-360"></a>第三百六十条　实现票据、仓单、提单等有权利凭证的权利质权案件，可以由权利凭证持有人住所地人民法院管辖；无权利凭证的权利质权，由出质登记地人民法院管辖。

**Article 360** . Cases for realizing a pledge of rights with right certificates such as negotiable instruments, warehouse receipts, or bills of lading may be under the jurisdiction of the People's Court at the domicile of the holder of the right certificate; for a pledge of rights without right certificates, the People's Court at the place of pledge registration shall have jurisdiction.

<a id="art-361"></a>第三百六十一条　实现担保物权案件属于海事法院等专门人民法院管辖的，由专门人民法院管辖。

**Article 361** . Where a case for realizing a security interest falls under the jurisdiction of a special People's Court such as a maritime court, the special People's Court shall have jurisdiction.

<a id="art-362"></a>第三百六十二条　同一债权的担保物有多个且所在地不同，申请人分别向有管辖权的人民法院申请实现担保物权的，人民法院应当依法受理。

**Article 362** . Where the security property for the same claim is multiple and located in different places, and the applicant applies separately to People's Courts with jurisdiction for realization of the security interest, the People's Courts shall accept the applications according to law.

<a id="art-363"></a>第三百六十三条　依照[民法典第三百九十二条](../../民事与商事/民法典/中华人民共和国民法典.md#art-392)的规定，被担保的债权既有物的担保又有人的担保，当事人对实现担保物权的顺序有约定，实现担保物权的申请违反该约定的，人民法院裁定不予受理；没有约定或者约定不明的，人民法院应当受理。

**Article 363** . In accordance with Article 392 of the Civil Code, where the secured claim is guaranteed by both security in kind and guarantee by a person, and the parties have agreed on the order of realizing the security interest, if an application for realizing the security interest violates that agreement, the People's Court shall rule not to accept the application; if there is no agreement or the agreement is unclear, the People's Court shall accept the application.

<a id="art-364"></a>第三百六十四条　同一财产上设立多个担保物权，登记在先的担保物权尚未实现的，不影响后顺位的担保物权人向人民法院申请实现担保物权。

**Article 364** . Where multiple security interests are established on the same property, the fact that a security interest registered earlier has not yet been realized shall not affect a security interest holder of a later priority from applying to the People's Court for realization of the security interest.

<a id="art-365"></a>第三百六十五条　申请实现担保物权，应当提交下列材料：  
(一)申请书。申请书应当记明申请人、被申请人的姓名或者名称、联系方式等基本信息，具体的请求和事实、理由；  
(二)证明担保物权存在的材料，包括主合同、担保合同、抵押登记证明或者他项权利证书，权利质权的权利凭证或者质权出质登记证明等；  
(三)证明实现担保物权条件成就的材料；  
(四)担保财产现状的说明；  
(五)人民法院认为需要提交的其他材料。

**Article 365** . To apply for realization of a security interest, the following materials shall be submitted:  
(1) A written application. The application shall state the basic information such as the names or titles and contact information of the applicant and the respondent, the specific claims, and the facts and reasons;  
(2) Materials proving the existence of the security interest, including the main contract, the security contract, mortgage registration certificate or certificate of other rights, right certificates for pledge of rights, or pledge registration certificate, etc.;  
(3) Materials proving that the conditions for realization of the security interest have been satisfied;  
(4) A description of the current status of the security property;  
(5) Other materials that the People's Court considers necessary to be submitted.

<a id="art-366"></a>第三百六十六条　人民法院受理申请后，应当在五日内向被申请人送达申请书副本、异议权利告知书等文书。  
被申请人有异议的，应当在收到人民法院通知后的五日内向人民法院提出，同时说明理由并提供相应的证据材料。

**Article 366** . After accepting the application, the People's Court shall, within five days, serve on the respondent copies of the application, notice of the right to raise objections, and other documents.  
If the respondent has objections, he or she shall raise them with the People's Court within five days after receipt of the notice from the People's Court, stating the reasons and providing corresponding evidentiary materials.

<a id="art-367"></a>第三百六十七条　实现担保物权案件可以由审判员一人独任审查。担保财产标的额超过基层人民法院管辖范围的，应当组成合议庭进行审查。

**Article 367** . A case for realizing a security interest may be examined by a single judge. Where the subject amount of the security property exceeds the jurisdictional limit of a primary People's Court, a collegiate bench shall be formed for examination.

<a id="art-368"></a>第三百六十八条　人民法院审查实现担保物权案件，可以询问申请人、被申请人、利害关系人，必要时可以依职权调查相关事实。

**Article 368** . When examining a case for realizing a security interest, the People's Court may question the applicant, the respondent, and interested parties, and if necessary, may investigate the relevant facts ex officio.

<a id="art-369"></a>第三百六十九条　人民法院应当就主合同的效力、期限、履行情况，担保物权是否有效设立、担保财产的范围、被担保的债权范围、被担保的债权是否已届清偿期等担保物权实现的条件，以及是否损害他人合法权益等内容进行审查。  
被申请人或者利害关系人提出异议的，人民法院应当一并审查。

**Article 369** . The People's Court shall examine the validity, term, and performance of the main contract, whether the security interest was validly established, the scope of the security property, the scope of the secured claim, whether the secured claim has become due, and other conditions for realization of the security interest, as well as whether the lawful rights and interests of others are harmed.  
Where the respondent or an interested party raises objections, the People's Court shall examine them together.

<a id="art-370"></a>第三百七十条　人民法院审查后，按下列情形分别处理：  
(一)当事人对实现担保物权无实质性争议且实现担保物权条件成就的，裁定准许拍卖、变卖担保财产；  
(二)当事人对实现担保物权有部分实质性争议的，可以就无争议部分裁定准许拍卖、变卖担保财产；  
(三)当事人对实现担保物权有实质性争议的，裁定驳回申请，并告知申请人向人民法院提起诉讼。

**Article 370** . After examination, the People's Court shall handle the case separately according to the following circumstances:  
(1) If the parties have no substantive dispute over the realization of the security interest and the conditions for realization are satisfied, it shall rule to permit the auction or sale of the security property;  
(2) If the parties have partial substantive disputes over the realization of the security interest, it may rule to permit the auction or sale of the security property with respect to the undisputed part;  
(3) If the parties have substantive disputes over the realization of the security interest, it shall rule to reject the application and inform the applicant to file an action with the People's Court.

<a id="art-371"></a>第三百七十一条　人民法院受理申请后，申请人对担保财产提出保全申请的，可以按照民事诉讼法关于诉讼保全的规定办理。

**Article 371** . After the People's Court accepts the application, if the applicant applies for property preservation of the security property, the matter may be handled in accordance with the provisions of the Civil Procedure Law on preservation in litigation.

<a id="art-372"></a>第三百七十二条　适用特别程序作出的判决、裁定，当事人、利害关系人认为有错误的，可以向作出该判决、裁定的人民法院提出异议。人民法院经审查，异议成立或者部分成立的，作出新的判决、裁定撤销或者改变原判决、裁定；异议不成立的，裁定驳回。  
对人民法院作出的确认调解协议、准许实现担保物权的裁定，当事人有异议的，应当自收到裁定之日起十五日内提出；利害关系人有异议的，自知道或者应当知道其民事权益受到侵害之日起六个月内提出。

**Article 372** . Where a party or interested party considers that a judgment or ruling made under the special procedure is erroneous, he or she may raise an objection with the People's Court that made the judgment or ruling. Upon examination by the People's Court, if the objection is established or partially established, a new judgment or ruling shall be made to revoke or modify the original judgment or ruling; if the objection is not established, it shall be rejected by ruling.  
Where a party has objections to a ruling made by the People's Court confirming a mediation agreement or permitting realization of a security interest, the party shall raise the objection within 15 days from receipt of the ruling; where an interested party has objections, the interested party shall raise the objection within six months from the date on which he or she knew or should have known that his or her civil rights and interests were infringed.

### 十八、审判监督程序

<a id="art-373"></a>第三百七十三条　当事人死亡或者终止的，其权利义务承继者可以根据[民事诉讼法第二百零六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-206)、第二百零八条的规定申请再审。  
判决、调解书生效后，当事人将判决、调解书确认的债权转让，债权受让人对该判决、调解书不服申请再审的，人民法院不予受理。

**Article 373** . Where a party dies or terminates, his or her successor in rights and obligations may apply for retrial in accordance with Articles 206 and 208 of the Civil Procedure Law.  
After a judgment or mediation statement takes effect, if the party transfers the claim confirmed by the judgment or mediation statement, and the assignee of the claim applies for retrial because he or she disagrees with the judgment or mediation statement, the People's Court shall not accept the application.

<a id="art-374"></a>第三百七十四条　[民事诉讼法第二百零六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-206)规定的人数众多的一方当事人，包括公民、法人和其他组织。  
[民事诉讼法第二百零六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-206)规定的当事人双方为公民的案件，是指原告和被告均为公民的案件。

**Article 374** . The "party with a large number of persons" as provided in Article 206 of the Civil Procedure Law includes citizens, legal persons, and other organizations.  
The case "where both parties are citizens" as provided in Article 206 of the Civil Procedure Law refers to a case in which both the plaintiff and the defendant are citizens.

<a id="art-375"></a>第三百七十五条　当事人申请再审，应当提交下列材料：  
(一)再审申请书，并按照被申请人和原审其他当事人的人数提交副本；  
(二)再审申请人是自然人的，应当提交身份证明；再审申请人是法人或者其他组织的，应当提交营业执照、组织机构代码证书、法定代表人或者主要负责人身份证明书。委托他人代为申请的，应当提交授权委托书和代理人身份证明；  
(三)原审判决书、裁定书、调解书；  
(四)反映案件基本事实的主要证据及其他材料。  
前款第二项、第三项、第四项规定的材料可以是与原件核对无异的复印件。

**Article 375** . A party applying for retrial shall submit the following materials:  
(1) A written application for retrial, together with copies in the number equal to the number of respondents and other parties in the original trial;  
(2) If the applicant for retrial is a natural person, he or she shall submit an identity certificate; if the applicant for retrial is a legal person or other organization, it shall submit a business license, organization code certificate, and certification of the identity of the legal representative or the principal person in charge. If another person is entrusted to make the application, a power of attorney and the agent's identity certificate shall be submitted;  
(3) The judgment, ruling, or mediation statement of the original trial;  
(4) The main evidence reflecting the basic facts of the case and other materials.  
The materials provided in items (2), (3), and (4) of the preceding paragraph may be copies that have been verified against the originals.

<a id="art-376"></a>第三百七十六条　再审申请书应当记明下列事项：  
(一)再审申请人与被申请人及原审其他当事人的基本信息；  
(二)原审人民法院的名称，原审裁判文书案号；  
(三)具体的再审请求；  
(四)申请再审的法定情形及具体事实、理由。  
再审申请书应当明确申请再审的人民法院，并由再审申请人签名、捺印或者盖章。

**Article 376** . A written application for retrial shall state the following matters:  
(1) The basic information of the applicant for retrial, the respondent, and other parties in the original trial;  
(2) The name of the People's Court of the original trial and the case number of the original adjudication document;  
(3) The specific claims for retrial;  
(4) The statutory circumstances for applying for retrial and the specific facts and reasons.  
The written application for retrial shall identify the People's Court to which the application for retrial is made, and shall be signed, fingerprinted, or sealed by the applicant for retrial.

<a id="art-377"></a>第三百七十七条　当事人一方人数众多或者当事人双方为公民的案件，当事人分别向原审人民法院和上一级人民法院申请再审且不能协商一致的，由原审人民法院受理。

**Article 377** . In a case where one party has a large number of persons or both parties are citizens, if the parties separately apply for retrial to the People's Court of the original trial and the People's Court at the next higher level and cannot reach an agreement, the People's Court of the original trial shall accept the application.

<a id="art-378"></a>第三百七十八条　适用特别程序、督促程序、公示催告程序、破产程序等非讼程序审理的案件，当事人不得申请再审。

**Article 378** . For cases adjudicated under non-contentious procedures such as the special procedure, summary procedure for urging debt repayment, procedure for public summons, and bankruptcy procedure, the parties may not apply for retrial.

<a id="art-379"></a>第三百七十九条　当事人认为发生法律效力的不予受理、驳回起诉的裁定错误的，可以申请再审。

**Article 379** . Where a party considers that a legally effective ruling of non-acceptance or rejection of prosecution is erroneous, the party may apply for retrial.

<a id="art-380"></a>第三百八十条　当事人就离婚案件中的财产分割问题申请再审，如涉及判决中已分割的财产，人民法院应当依照[民事诉讼法第二百零七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-207)的规定进行审查，符合再审条件的，应当裁定再审；如涉及判决中未作处理的夫妻共同财产，应当告知当事人另行起诉。

**Article 380** . Where a party applies for retrial regarding the division of property in a divorce case, if it involves property already divided in the judgment, the People's Court shall examine the matter in accordance with Article 207 of the Civil Procedure Law, and if the conditions for retrial are met, shall rule for retrial; if it involves community property of the husband and wife not addressed in the judgment, the parties shall be informed to file a separate action.

<a id="art-381"></a>第三百八十一条　当事人申请再审，有下列情形之一的，人民法院不予受理：  
(一)再审申请被驳回后再次提出申请的；  
(二)对再审判决、裁定提出申请的；  
(三)在人民检察院对当事人的申请作出不予提出再审检察建议或者抗诉决定后又提出申请的。  
前款第一项、第二项规定情形，人民法院应当告知当事人可以向人民检察院申请再审检察建议或者抗诉，但因人民检察院提出再审检察建议或者抗诉而再审作出的判决、裁定除外。

**Article 381** . Where a party applies for retrial, in any of the following circumstances, the People's Court shall not accept the application:  
(1) An application is filed again after a previous application for retrial has been rejected;  
(2) An application is filed against a retrial judgment or ruling;  
(3) An application is filed again after the People's Procuratorate has made a decision not to issue a procuratorial recommendation for retrial or to lodge a protest against the party's application.  
In the circumstances provided for in items (1) and (2) of the preceding paragraph, the People's Court shall inform the party that he or she may apply to the People's Procuratorate for a procuratorial recommendation for retrial or a protest, except for judgments or rulings made upon retrial due to a procuratorial recommendation for retrial or protest lodged by the People's Procuratorate.

<a id="art-382"></a>第三百八十二条　当事人对已经发生法律效力的调解书申请再审，应当在调解书发生法律效力后六个月内提出。

**Article 382** . A party applying for retrial against a mediation statement that has already taken legal effect shall file the application within six months after the mediation statement takes legal effect.

<a id="art-383"></a>第三百八十三条　人民法院应当自收到符合条件的再审申请书等材料之日起五日内向再审申请人发送受理通知书，并向被申请人及原审其他当事人发送应诉通知书、再审申请书副本等材料。

**Article 383** . The People's Court shall, within five days from receipt of qualified materials such as the application for retrial, send a notice of acceptance to the applicant for retrial, and send a notice to respond to the action, a copy of the application for retrial, and other materials to the respondent and the other parties in the original trial.

<a id="art-384"></a>第三百八十四条　人民法院受理申请再审案件后，应当依照[民事诉讼法第二百零七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-207)、第二百零八条、第二百一十一条等规定，对当事人主张的再审事由进行审查。

**Article 384** . After accepting a case of application for retrial, the People's Court shall, in accordance with the provisions of Articles 207, 208, and 211 of the Civil Procedure Law, examine the grounds for retrial asserted by the parties.

<a id="art-385"></a>第三百八十五条　再审申请人提供的新的证据，能够证明原判决、裁定认定基本事实或者裁判结果错误的，应当认定为[民事诉讼法第二百零七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-207)第一项规定的情形。  
对于符合前款规定的证据，人民法院应当责令再审申请人说明其逾期提供该证据的理由；拒不说明理由或者理由不成立的，依照[民事诉讼法第六十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-68)第二款和[本解释第一百零二条](#art-102)的规定处理。

**Article 385** . If the new evidence provided by the applicant for retrial can prove that the basic facts determined in the original judgment or ruling or the adjudication result are erroneous, it shall be determined as the circumstance provided for in item (1) of Article 207 of the Civil Procedure Law.  
With respect to evidence that complies with the provisions of the preceding paragraph, the People's Court shall order the applicant for retrial to explain the reasons for providing the evidence belatedly; if the applicant refuses to explain the reasons or the reasons are not established, the matter shall be handled in accordance with paragraph 2 of Article 68 of the Civil Procedure Law and Article 102 of this Interpretation.

<a id="art-386"></a>第三百八十六条　再审申请人证明其提交的新的证据符合下列情形之一的，可以认定逾期提供证据的理由成立：  
(一)在原审庭审结束前已经存在，因客观原因于庭审结束后才发现的；  
(二)在原审庭审结束前已经发现，但因客观原因无法取得或者在规定的期限内不能提供的；  
(三)在原审庭审结束后形成，无法据此另行提起诉讼的。  
再审申请人提交的证据在原审中已经提供，原审人民法院未组织质证且未作为裁判根据的，视为逾期提供证据的理由成立，但原审人民法院依照[民事诉讼法第六十八条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-68)规定不予采纳的除外。

**Article 386** . Where the applicant for retrial proves that the new evidence submitted by him or her falls under any of the following circumstances, the reasons for belated provision of evidence may be found to be established:  
(1) The evidence existed before the conclusion of the hearing in the original trial, but was only discovered after the hearing due to objective reasons;  
(2) The evidence was discovered before the conclusion of the hearing in the original trial, but could not be obtained due to objective reasons or could not be provided within the prescribed time limit;  
(3) The evidence was formed after the conclusion of the hearing in the original trial, and no separate action can be brought based on it.  
Where the evidence submitted by the applicant for retrial had already been provided in the original trial, and the People's Court of the original trial did not organize cross-examination and did not use it as the basis for adjudication, the reasons for belated provision of evidence shall be deemed established, unless the People's Court of the original trial did not admit it in accordance with Article 68 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-387"></a>第三百八十七条　当事人对原判决、裁定认定事实的主要证据在原审中拒绝发表质证意见或者质证中未对证据发表质证意见的，不属于[民事诉讼法第二百零七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-207)第四项规定的未经质证的情形。

**Article 387** . Where a party, with respect to the main evidence for determining the facts in the original judgment or ruling, refused to express cross-examination opinions during the original trial or did not express cross-examination opinions on the evidence during cross-examination, this shall not constitute the circumstance of "without cross-examination" provided for in item (4) of Article 207 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-388"></a>第三百八十八条　有下列情形之一，导致判决、裁定结果错误的，应当认定为[民事诉讼法第二百零七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-207)第六项规定的原判决、裁定适用法律确有错误：  
(一)适用的法律与案件性质明显不符的；  
(二)确定民事责任明显违背当事人约定或者法律规定的；  
(三)适用已经失效或者尚未施行的法律的；  
(四)违反法律溯及力规定的；  
(五)违反法律适用规则的；  
(六)明显违背立法原意的。

**Article 388** . Any of the following circumstances resulting in an erroneous outcome of a judgment or ruling shall be determined as the "definite error in the application of law in the original judgment or ruling" provided for in item (6) of Article 207 of the Civil Procedure Law:  
(1) The applied law is obviously inconsistent with the nature of the case;  
(2) The determination of civil liability obviously violates the parties' agreement or legal provisions;  
(3) A law that has already ceased to be effective or has not yet come into force is applied;  
(4) Provisions on the retroactive effect of laws are violated;  
(5) Rules on the application of law are violated;  
(6) The legislative intent is obviously contravened.

<a id="art-389"></a>第三百八十九条　原审开庭过程中有下列情形之一的，应当认定为[民事诉讼法第二百零七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-207)第九项规定的剥夺当事人辩论权利：  
（一）不允许当事人发表辩论意见的；  
（二）应当开庭审理而未开庭审理的；  
（三）违反法律规定送达起诉状副本或者上诉状副本，致使当事人无法行使辩论权利的；  
（四）违法剥夺当事人辩论权利的其他情形。

**Article 389** . Any of the following circumstances in the course of the hearing in the original trial shall be determined as "depriving the parties of the right to debate" provided for in item (9) of Article 207 of the Civil Procedure Law:  
(1) The parties are not allowed to express their debate opinions;  
(2) The case should have been heard in court but was not heard in court;  
(3) The service of the copy of the statement of complaint or the copy of the statement of appeal violates legal provisions, thereby causing the parties to be unable to exercise their right to debate;  
(4) Other circumstances in which the parties' right to debate is illegally deprived.

<a id="art-390"></a>第三百九十条　[民事诉讼法第二百零七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-207)第十一项规定的诉讼请求，包括一审诉讼请求、二审上诉请求，但当事人未对一审判决、裁定遗漏或者超出诉讼请求提起上诉的除外。

**Article 390** . The "litigation claims" provided for in item (11) of Article 207 of the Civil Procedure Law include the litigation claims in the first instance and the appellate claims in the second instance, except where the party did not appeal against the omission of or excess over the litigation claims in the first-instance judgment or ruling.

<a id="art-391"></a>第三百九十一条　[民事诉讼法第二百零七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-207)第十二项规定的法律文书包括：  
(一)发生法律效力的判决书、裁定书、调解书；  
(二)发生法律效力的仲裁裁决书；  
(三)具有强制执行效力的公证债权文书。

**Article 391** . The "legal documents" provided for in item (12) of Article 207 of the Civil Procedure Law include:  
(1) Judgments, rulings, and mediation statements that have taken legal effect;  
(2) Arbitral awards that have taken legal effect;  
(3) Notarized debt documents with compulsory execution effect.

<a id="art-392"></a>第三百九十二条　[民事诉讼法第二百零七条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-207)第十三项规定的审判人员审理该案件时有贪污受贿、徇私舞弊、枉法裁判行为，是指已经由生效刑事法律文书或者纪律处分决定所确认的行为。

**Article 392** .
