**Article 510** Where a creditor or the person subject to execution submits a written objection to the distribution plan, the executing court shall notify the creditors and the person subject to execution who have not raised an objection.  
If the creditors and the person subject to execution who have not raised an objection do not raise any opposing opinions within fifteen days from the date of receipt of the notice, the executing court shall, in accordance with the opinions of the objecting person, review and amend the distribution plan and then make the distribution; if any opposing opinion is raised, the objecting person shall be notified. The objecting person may, within fifteen days from the date of receipt of the notice, file an action with the executing court against the creditors or the person subject to execution who raised the opposing opinion as defendants; if the objecting person fails to file an action within the prescribed period, the executing court shall make distribution in accordance with the original distribution plan.  
Where distribution is made during the pendency of the action, the executing court shall deposit an amount of money corresponding to the disputed claim.

<a id="art-511"></a>第五百一十一条　在执行中，作为被执行人的企业法人符合[企业破产法第二条](../../民事与商事/公司与破产/中华人民共和国企业破产法.md#art-2)第一款规定情形的，执行法院经申请执行人之一或者被执行人同意，应当裁定中止对该被执行人的执行，将执行案件相关材料移送被执行人住所地人民法院。

**Article 511** Where, during enforcement, an enterprise legal person subject to execution falls under the circumstances specified in Paragraph 1 of Article 2 of the Enterprise Bankruptcy Law, the executing court shall, with the consent of one of the applicants for execution or the person subject to execution, rule to suspend the enforcement against such person subject to execution and transfer the relevant materials of the enforcement case to the People's Court at the place of domicile of the person subject to execution.

<a id="art-512"></a>第五百一十二条　被执行人住所地人民法院应当自收到执行案件相关材料之日起三十日内，将是否受理破产案件的裁定告知执行法院。不予受理的，应当将相关案件材料退回执行法院。

**Article 512** The People's Court at the place of domicile of the person subject to execution shall, within thirty days from the date of receipt of the relevant materials of the enforcement case, notify the executing court of its ruling on whether to accept the bankruptcy case. If it decides not to accept the case, it shall return the relevant case materials to the executing court.

<a id="art-513"></a>第五百一十三条　被执行人住所地人民法院裁定受理破产案件的，执行法院应当解除对被执行人财产的保全措施。被执行人住所地人民法院裁定宣告被执行人破产的，执行法院应当裁定终结对该被执行人的执行。  
被执行人住所地人民法院不受理破产案件的，执行法院应当恢复执行。

**Article 513** Where the People's Court at the place of domicile of the person subject to execution rules to accept the bankruptcy case, the executing court shall lift the preservation measures on the property of the person subject to execution. Where the People's Court at the place of domicile of the person subject to execution declares the person subject to execution bankrupt, the executing court shall rule to terminate the enforcement against such person.  
Where the People's Court at the place of domicile of the person subject to execution does not accept the bankruptcy case, the executing court shall resume enforcement.

<a id="art-514"></a>第五百一十四条　当事人不同意移送破产或者被执行人住所地人民法院不受理破产案件的，执行法院就执行变价所得财产，在扣除执行费用及清偿优先受偿的债权后，对于普通债权，按照财产保全和执行中查封、扣押、冻结财产的先后顺序清偿。

**Article 514** Where the parties do not agree to the transfer of the case to bankruptcy, or the People's Court at the place of domicile of the person subject to execution does not accept the bankruptcy case, the executing court shall, with respect to the proceeds obtained from the realization of property in enforcement, after deducting enforcement expenses and satisfying claims entitled to priority repayment, satisfy ordinary claims in the chronological order of the sealing up, seizure, or freezing of the property during property preservation and enforcement.

<a id="art-515"></a>第五百一十五条　债权人根据[民事诉讼法第二百六十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-261)规定请求人民法院继续执行的，不受[民事诉讼法第二百四十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-246)规定申请执行时效期间的限制。

**Article 515** Where a creditor requests the People's Court to continue enforcement in accordance with Article 261 of the Civil Procedure Law, the creditor shall not be subject to the limitation period for applying for enforcement as provided in Article 246 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-516"></a>第五百一十六条　被执行人不履行法律文书确定的义务的，人民法院除对被执行人予以处罚外，还可以根据情节将其纳入失信被执行人名单，将被执行人不履行或者不完全履行义务的信息向其所在单位、征信机构以及其他相关机构通报。

**Article 516** Where the person subject to execution fails to perform the obligations determined in a legal instrument, the People's Court may, in addition to imposing a penalty on the person subject to execution, include the person in the list of dishonest persons subject to execution according to the circumstances, and notify the person's work unit, credit reference agencies, and other relevant institutions of the information that the person subject to execution has failed or incompletely performed the obligations.

<a id="art-517"></a>第五百一十七条　经过财产调查未发现可供执行的财产，在申请执行人签字确认或者执行法院组成合议庭审查核实并经院长批准后，可以裁定终结本次执行程序。  
依照前款规定终结执行后，申请执行人发现被执行人有可供执行财产的，可以再次申请执行。再次申请不受申请执行时效期间的限制。

**Article 517** Where no property available for enforcement is discovered after a property investigation, the executing court may, upon the applicant for execution's signature for confirmation, or after the executing court forms a collegiate bench to examine and verify the matter and obtains the approval of the president of the court, rule to conclude the current enforcement proceedings.  
After the enforcement is concluded in accordance with the preceding paragraph, if the applicant for execution discovers that the person subject to execution has property available for enforcement, the applicant may apply for enforcement again. A re-application shall not be subject to the limitation period for applying for enforcement.

<a id="art-518"></a>第五百一十八条　因撤销申请而终结执行后，当事人在[民事诉讼法第二百四十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-246)规定的申请执行时效期间内再次申请执行的，人民法院应当受理。

**Article 518** Where enforcement is concluded due to the withdrawal of the application, if a party applies for enforcement again within the limitation period for applying for enforcement as provided in Article 246 of the Civil Procedure Law, the People's Court shall accept the application.

<a id="art-519"></a>第五百一十九条　在执行终结六个月内，被执行人或者其他人对已执行的标的有妨害行为的，人民法院可以依申请排除妨害，并可以依照[民事诉讼法第一百一十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-114)规定进行处罚。因妨害行为给执行债权人或者其他人造成损失的，受害人可以另行起诉。

**Article 519** Where, within six months after the conclusion of enforcement, the person subject to execution or any other person commits an act obstructing the subject matter already enforced upon, the People's Court may, upon application, remove the obstruction, and may impose a penalty in accordance with Article 114 of the Civil Procedure Law. Where losses are caused to the enforcement creditor or other persons by the obstructive act, the victim may separately file an action.

### 二十二、涉外民事诉讼程序的特别规定

<a id="art-520"></a>第五百二十条　有下列情形之一，人民法院可以认定为涉外民事案件：  
(一)当事人一方或者双方是外国人、无国籍人、外国企业或者组织的；  
(二)当事人一方或者双方的经常居所地在中华人民共和国领域外的；  
(三)标的物在中华人民共和国领域外的；  
(四)产生、变更或者消灭民事关系的法律事实发生在中华人民共和国领域外的；  
(五)可以认定为涉外民事案件的其他情形。

**Article 520** The People's Court may determine a case to be a foreign-related civil case under any of the following circumstances:  
(1) One or both of the parties are foreigners, stateless persons, foreign enterprises, or organizations;  
(2) The habitual residence of one or both of the parties is outside the territory of the People's Republic of China;  
(3) The subject matter is located outside the territory of the People's Republic of China;  
(4) The legal facts giving rise to, changing, or extinguishing civil relations occur outside the territory of the People's Republic of China; or  
(5) Other circumstances under which a case may be determined as a foreign-related civil case.

<a id="art-521"></a>第五百二十一条　外国人参加诉讼，应当向人民法院提交护照等用以证明自己身份的证件。  
外国企业或者组织参加诉讼，向人民法院提交的身份证明文件，应当经所在国公证机关公证，并经中华人民共和国驻该国使领馆认证，或者履行中华人民共和国与该所在国订立的有关条约中规定的证明手续。  
代表外国企业或者组织参加诉讼的人，应当向人民法院提交其有权作为代表人参加诉讼的证明，该证明应当经所在国公证机关公证，并经中华人民共和国驻该国使领馆认证，或者履行中华人民共和国与该所在国订立的有关条约中规定的证明手续。  
本条所称的“所在国”，是指外国企业或者组织的设立登记地国，也可以是办理了营业登记手续的第三国。

**Article 521** A foreigner participating in litigation shall submit to the People's Court his or her passport or other identification documents used to prove his or her identity.  
Where a foreign enterprise or organization participates in litigation, the identification documents submitted to the People's Court shall be notarized by the notarization authority of the country where it is located and legalized by the embassy or consulate of the People's Republic of China in that country, or the certification formalities prescribed in the relevant treaties concluded between the People's Republic of China and that country shall be fulfilled.  
A person representing a foreign enterprise or organization in litigation shall submit to the People's Court proof that he or she is entitled to participate in litigation as a representative; such proof shall be notarized by the notarization authority of the country where it is located and legalized by the embassy or consulate of the People's Republic of China in that country, or the certification formalities prescribed in the relevant treaties concluded between the People's Republic of China and that country shall be fulfilled.  
For the purposes of this Article, the "country where it is located" means the country where the foreign enterprise or organization is registered for establishment, or a third country where the enterprise or organization has completed business registration formalities.

<a id="art-522"></a>第五百二十二条　依照[民事诉讼法第二百七十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-271)以及[本解释第五百二十一条](#art-521)规定，需要办理公证、认证手续，而外国当事人所在国与中华人民共和国没有建立外交关系的，可以经该国公证机关公证，经与中华人民共和国有外交关系的第三国驻该国使领馆认证，再转由中华人民共和国驻该第三国使领馆认证。

**Article 522** Where, in accordance with Article 271 of the Civil Procedure Law and Article 521 of this Interpretation, notarization and legalization formalities need to be completed, but the country where a foreign party is located has not established diplomatic relations with the People's Republic of China, the documents may be notarized by the notarization authority of that country, legalized by the embassy or consulate of a third country that has diplomatic relations with the People's Republic of China and is stationed in that country, and then forwarded to the embassy or consulate of the People's Republic of China in that third country for legalization.

<a id="art-523"></a>第五百二十三条　外国人、外国企业或者组织的代表人在人民法院法官的见证下签署授权委托书，委托代理人进行民事诉讼的，人民法院应予认可。

**Article 523** Where a foreigner, or a representative of a foreign enterprise or organization, signs a power of attorney in the presence of a judge of the People's Court, authorizing an agent to conduct a civil action, the People's Court shall recognize such authorization.

<a id="art-524"></a>第五百二十四条　外国人、外国企业或者组织的代表人在中华人民共和国境内签署授权委托书，委托代理人进行民事诉讼，经中华人民共和国公证机构公证的，人民法院应予认可。

**Article 524** Where a foreigner, or a representative of a foreign enterprise or organization, signs a power of attorney within the territory of the People's Republic of China, authorizing an agent to conduct a civil action, and the power of attorney is notarized by a notarization authority of the People's Republic of China, the People's Court shall recognize such authorization.

<a id="art-525"></a>第五百二十五条　当事人向人民法院提交的书面材料是外文的，应当同时向人民法院提交中文翻译件。  
当事人对中文翻译件有异议的，应当共同委托翻译机构提供翻译文本；当事人对翻译机构的选择不能达成一致的，由人民法院确定。

**Article 525** Where a party submits written material in a foreign language to the People's Court, the party shall simultaneously submit a Chinese translation to the People's Court.  
If a party has an objection to the Chinese translation, the parties shall jointly entrust a translation institution to provide a translated text; if the parties cannot reach an agreement on the choice of the translation institution, the People's Court shall designate one.

<a id="art-526"></a>第五百二十六条　涉外民事诉讼中的外籍当事人，可以委托本国人为诉讼代理人，也可以委托本国律师以非律师身份担任诉讼代理人；外国驻华使领馆官员，受本国公民的委托，可以以个人名义担任诉讼代理人，但在诉讼中不享有外交或者领事特权和豁免。

**Article 526** In foreign-related civil litigation, a foreign party may entrust a national of his or her own country as a litigation agent, and may also entrust a lawyer of his or her own country to serve as a litigation agent in a non-lawyer capacity; officials of foreign embassies or consulates in China, upon entrustment by their own nationals, may serve as litigation agents in their personal names, but shall not enjoy diplomatic or consular privileges and immunities in the litigation.

<a id="art-527"></a>第五百二十七条　涉外民事诉讼中，外国驻华使领馆授权其本馆官员，在作为当事人的本国国民不在中华人民共和国领域内的情况下，可以以外交代表身份为其本国国民在中华人民共和国聘请中华人民共和国律师或者中华人民共和国公民代理民事诉讼。

**Article 527** In foreign-related civil litigation, where a foreign embassy or consulate in China authorizes an officer of such embassy or consulate, and the national of that country who is a party is not within the territory of the People's Republic of China, the officer may, in the capacity of a diplomatic representative, engage a lawyer of the People's Republic of China or a citizen of the People's Republic of China, on behalf of such national, to act as an agent in the civil litigation.

<a id="art-528"></a>第五百二十八条　涉外民事诉讼中，经调解双方达成协议，应当制发调解书。当事人要求发给判决书的，可以依协议的内容制作判决书送达当事人。

**Article 528** In foreign-related civil litigation, if the parties reach an agreement through mediation, a mediation statement shall be prepared and issued. If a party requests a judgment, a judgment may be prepared in accordance with the content of the agreement and served upon the parties.

<a id="art-529"></a>第五百二十九条　涉外合同或者其他财产权益纠纷的当事人，可以书面协议选择被告住所地、合同履行地、合同签订地、原告住所地、标的物所在地、侵权行为地等与争议有实际联系地点的外国法院管辖。  
根据[民事诉讼法第三十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-34)和第二百七十三条规定，属于中华人民共和国法院专属管辖的案件，当事人不得协议选择外国法院管辖，但协议选择仲裁的除外。

**Article 529** Parties to a foreign-related contract or to other disputes over property rights and interests may, by written agreement, choose a foreign court at a place that has an actual connection with the dispute, such as the place of domicile of the defendant, the place of performance of the contract, the place where the contract is signed, the place of domicile of the plaintiff, the place where the subject matter is located, or the place where the tortious act is committed, to have jurisdiction.  
In accordance with the provisions of Article 34 and Article 273 of the Civil Procedure Law, for cases falling under the exclusive jurisdiction of the courts of the People's Republic of China, the parties shall not agree to choose a foreign court to exercise jurisdiction, except where the parties agree to choose arbitration.

<a id="art-530"></a>第五百三十条　涉外民事案件同时符合下列情形的，人民法院可以裁定驳回原告的起诉，告知其向更方便的外国法院提起诉讼：  
(一)被告提出案件应由更方便外国法院管辖的请求，或者提出管辖异议；  
(二)当事人之间不存在选择中华人民共和国法院管辖的协议；  
(三)案件不属于中华人民共和国法院专属管辖；  
(四)案件不涉及中华人民共和国国家、公民、法人或者其他组织的利益；  
(五)案件争议的主要事实不是发生在中华人民共和国境内，且案件不适用中华人民共和国法律，人民法院审理案件在认定事实和适用法律方面存在重大困难；  
(六)外国法院对案件享有管辖权，且审理该案件更加方便。

**Article 530** Where a foreign-related civil case satisfies all of the following circumstances, the People's Court may rule to dismiss the plaintiff's action and inform the plaintiff to bring the action in a more convenient foreign court:  
(1) The defendant requests that the case be under the jurisdiction of a more convenient foreign court, or raises an objection to jurisdiction;  
(2) There is no agreement between the parties selecting a court of the People's Republic of China for jurisdiction;  
(3) The case is not subject to the exclusive jurisdiction of the courts of the People's Republic of China;  
(4) The case does not involve the interests of the state, citizens, legal persons, or other organizations of the People's Republic of China;  
(5) The principal facts in dispute do not occur within the territory of the People's Republic of China, and the case is not governed by the law of the People's Republic of China, so that the People's Court would encounter significant difficulties in ascertaining the facts and applying the law in adjudicating the case; and  
(6) A foreign court has jurisdiction over the case, and it is more convenient for that court to adjudicate the case.

<a id="art-531"></a>第五百三十一条　中华人民共和国法院和外国法院都有管辖权的案件，一方当事人向外国法院起诉，而另一方当事人向中华人民共和国法院起诉的，人民法院可予受理。判决后，外国法院申请或者当事人请求人民法院承认和执行外国法院对本案作出的判决、裁定的，不予准许；但双方共同缔结或者参加的国际条约另有规定的除外。  
外国法院判决、裁定已经被人民法院承认，当事人就同一争议向人民法院起诉的，人民法院不予受理。

**Article 531** Where a case is within the jurisdiction of both the courts of the People's Republic of China and a foreign court, if one party commences an action in the foreign court while the other party commences an action in a court of the People's Republic of China, the People's Court may accept the case. After a judgment is rendered, if the foreign court applies or a party requests the People's Court to recognize and enforce the judgment or ruling rendered by the foreign court with respect to the case, such application or request shall not be granted, unless an international treaty concluded or acceded to by both parties provides otherwise.  
Where a judgment or ruling of a foreign court has already been recognized by the People's Court, if a party commences an action in the People's Court over the same dispute, the People's Court shall not accept the action.

<a id="art-532"></a>第五百三十二条　对在中华人民共和国领域内没有住所的当事人，经用公告方式送达诉讼文书，公告期满不应诉，人民法院缺席判决后，仍应当将裁判文书依照[民事诉讼法第二百七十四条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-274)第八项规定公告送达。自公告送达裁判文书满三个月之日起，经过三十日的上诉期当事人没有上诉的，一审判决即发生法律效力。

**Article 532** For a party who has no domicile within the territory of the People's Republic of China, where litigation documents are served by announcement and the party fails to appear in court upon the expiration of the announcement period, the People's Court may render a judgment by default, after which it shall still serve the judgment document by announcement in accordance with Item 8 of Paragraph 1 of Article 274 of the Civil Procedure Law. Where, upon the expiration of three months from the date of service of the judgment document by announcement, the party does not appeal within the thirty-day appeal period, the judgment of first instance shall become legally effective.

<a id="art-533"></a>第五百三十三条　外国人或者外国企业、组织的代表人、主要负责人在中华人民共和国领域内的，人民法院可以向该自然人或者外国企业、组织的代表人、主要负责人送达。  
外国企业、组织的主要负责人包括该企业、组织的董事、监事、高级管理人员等。

**Article 533** Where a foreign national, or the representative or principal responsible person of a foreign enterprise or organization, is within the territory of the People's Republic of China, the People's Court may serve documents on such natural person, or the representative or principal responsible person of the foreign enterprise or organization.  
The principal responsible persons of a foreign enterprise or organization include the directors, supervisors, senior officers, and other such personnel of the enterprise or organization.

<a id="art-534"></a>第五百三十四条　受送达人所在国允许邮寄送达的，人民法院可以邮寄送达。  
邮寄送达时应当附有送达回证。受送达人未在送达回证上签收但在邮件回执上签收的，视为送达，签收日期为送达日期。  
自邮寄之日起满三个月，如果未收到送达的证明文件，且根据各种情况不足以认定已经送达的，视为不能用邮寄方式送达。

**Article 534** Where the country where the person to be served is located permits service by mail, the People's Court may serve by mail.  
When serving by mail, an acknowledgment of service shall be enclosed. If the person to be served does not sign on the acknowledgment of service but signs on the mail receipt, the service shall be deemed to be effected, and the date of signing shall be the date of service.  
If, upon the expiration of three months from the date of mailing, no proof of service is received and it cannot be determined, based on the circumstances, that service has been effected, the service shall be deemed impossible by mail.

<a id="art-535"></a>第五百三十五条　人民法院一审时采取公告方式向当事人送达诉讼文书的，二审时可径行采取公告方式向其送达诉讼文书，但人民法院能够采取公告方式之外的其他方式送达的除外。

**Article 535** Where the People's Court serves litigation documents on a party by announcement in the first instance, it may directly serve litigation documents by announcement in the second instance, unless the People's Court is able to serve by other means than announcement.

<a id="art-536"></a>第五百三十六条　不服第一审人民法院判决、裁定的上诉期，对在中华人民共和国领域内有住所的当事人，适用[民事诉讼法第一百七十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-171)规定的期限；对在中华人民共和国领域内没有住所的当事人，适用[民事诉讼法第二百七十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-276)规定的期限。当事人的上诉期均已届满没有上诉的，第一审人民法院的判决、裁定即发生法律效力。

**Article 536** The appeal period for appealing against a judgment or ruling of a People's Court of first instance shall, for a party having a domicile within the territory of the People's Republic of China, be the period prescribed in Article 171 of the Civil Procedure Law; and for a party having no domicile within the territory of the People's Republic of China, be the period prescribed in Article 276 of the Civil Procedure Law. Where the appeal periods of all parties have expired without an appeal, the judgment or ruling of the People's Court of first instance shall become legally effective.

<a id="art-537"></a>第五百三十七条　人民法院对涉外民事案件的当事人申请再审进行审查的期间，不受[民事诉讼法第二百一十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-211)规定的限制。

**Article 537** The period for the People's Court to examine an application for retrial filed by a party to a foreign-related civil case shall not be subject to the restriction provided in Article 211 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-538"></a>第五百三十八条　申请人向人民法院申请执行中华人民共和国涉外仲裁机构的裁决，应当提出书面申请，并附裁决书正本。如申请人为外国当事人，其申请书应当用中文文本提出。

**Article 538** An applicant who applies to the People's Court for enforcement of an award of a foreign-related arbitration institution of the People's Republic of China shall submit a written application, together with the original of the award. If the applicant is a foreign party, the application shall be submitted in the Chinese language.

<a id="art-539"></a>第五百三十九条　人民法院强制执行涉外仲裁机构的仲裁裁决时，被执行人以有[民事诉讼法第二百八十一条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-281)第一款规定的情形为由提出抗辩的，人民法院应当对被执行人的抗辩进行审查，并根据审查结果裁定执行或者不予执行。

**Article 539** When the People's Court enforces an arbitral award of a foreign-related arbitration institution by compulsory execution, if the person subject to execution raises an objection on the ground that a circumstance provided in Paragraph 1 of Article 281 of the Civil Procedure Law exists, the People's Court shall examine the objection of the person subject to execution, and shall rule to enforce or not to enforce the award based on the result of the examination.

<a id="art-540"></a>第五百四十条　依照[民事诉讼法第二百七十九条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-279)规定，中华人民共和国涉外仲裁机构将当事人的保全申请提交人民法院裁定的，人民法院可以进行审查，裁定是否进行保全。裁定保全的，应当责令申请人提供担保，申请人不提供担保的，裁定驳回申请。  
当事人申请证据保全，人民法院经审查认为无需提供担保的，申请人可以不提供担保。

**Article 540** Where, in accordance with Article 279 of the Civil Procedure Law, a foreign-related arbitration institution of the People's Republic of China submits a party's application for preservation to the People's Court for a ruling, the People's Court may examine the application and rule on whether to grant preservation. If it rules to grant preservation, it shall order the applicant to provide security; if the applicant does not provide security, the court shall rule to dismiss the application.  
Where a party applies for preservation of evidence and the People's Court, upon examination, considers that providing security is unnecessary, the applicant may not provide security.

<a id="art-541"></a>第五百四十一条　申请人向人民法院申请承认和执行外国法院作出的发生法律效力的判决、裁定，应当提交申请书，并附外国法院作出的发生法律效力的判决、裁定正本或者经证明无误的副本以及中文译本。外国法院判决、裁定为缺席判决、裁定的，申请人应当同时提交该外国法院已经合法传唤的证明文件，但判决、裁定已经对此予以明确说明的除外。  
中华人民共和国缔结或者参加的国际条约对提交文件有规定的，按照规定办理。

**Article 541** An applicant who applies to the People's Court for recognition and enforcement of a legally effective judgment or ruling rendered by a foreign court shall submit an application, together with the original or a certified true copy of the legally effective judgment or ruling rendered by the foreign court, as well as a Chinese translation. Where the judgment or ruling of the foreign court is a default judgment or ruling, the applicant shall also submit evidentiary documents proving that the foreign court has lawfully summoned the party, unless the judgment or ruling already clearly states such fact.  
Where an international treaty concluded or acceded to by the People's Republic of China contains provisions on the submission of documents, such provisions shall apply.

<a id="art-542"></a>第五百四十二条　当事人向中华人民共和国有管辖权的中级人民法院申请承认和执行外国法院作出的发生法律效力的判决、裁定的，如果该法院所在国与中华人民共和国没有缔结或者共同参加国际条约，也没有互惠关系的，裁定驳回申请，但当事人向人民法院申请承认外国法院作出的发生法律效力的离婚判决的除外。  
承认和执行申请被裁定驳回的，当事人可以向人民法院起诉。

**Article 542** Where a party applies to an intermediate people's court of the People's Republic of China that has jurisdiction for recognition and enforcement of a legally effective judgment or ruling rendered by a foreign court, if the country where the foreign court is located has neither concluded nor jointly acceded to an international treaty with the People's Republic of China, nor established a reciprocal relationship, the court shall rule to dismiss the application, except where the party applies to the People's Court for recognition of a legally effective divorce judgment rendered by a foreign court.  
Where an application for recognition and enforcement is dismissed by ruling, the party may commence an action with the People's Court.

<a id="art-543"></a>第五百四十三条　对临时仲裁庭在中华人民共和国领域外作出的仲裁裁决，一方当事人向人民法院申请承认和执行的，人民法院应当依照[民事诉讼法第二百九十条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-290)规定处理。

**Article 543** Where a party applies to the People's Court for recognition and enforcement of an arbitral award rendered by an ad hoc arbitral tribunal outside the territory of the People's Republic of China, the People's Court shall handle the matter in accordance with Article 290 of the Civil Procedure Law.

<a id="art-544"></a>第五百四十四条　对外国法院作出的发生法律效力的判决、裁定或者外国仲裁裁决，需要中华人民共和国法院执行的，当事人应当先向人民法院申请承认。人民法院经审查，裁定承认后，再根据民事诉讼法第三编的规定予以执行。  
当事人仅申请承认而未同时申请执行的，人民法院仅对应否承认进行审查并作出裁定。

**Article 544** Where a legally effective judgment or ruling rendered by a foreign court or a foreign arbitral award requires enforcement by a court of the People's Republic of China, the party shall first apply to the People's Court for recognition. After the People's Court examines the matter and rules to recognize, the judgment, ruling, or award shall be enforced in accordance with the provisions of Part III of the Civil Procedure Law.  
Where a party only applies for recognition and does not simultaneously apply for enforcement, the People's Court shall only examine whether recognition shall be granted and render a ruling accordingly.

<a id="art-545"></a>第五百四十五条　当事人申请承认和执行外国法院作出的发生法律效力的判决、裁定或者外国仲裁裁决的期间，适用[民事诉讼法第二百四十六条](../../诉讼与司法程序/民事诉讼/中华人民共和国民事诉讼法.md#art-246)的规定。  
当事人仅申请承认而未同时申请执行的，申请执行的期间自人民法院对承认申请作出的裁定生效之日起重新计算。

**Article 545** The period for a party to apply for recognition and enforcement of a legally effective judgment or ruling rendered by a foreign court or a foreign arbitral award shall be governed by Article 246 of the Civil Procedure Law.  
Where a party only applies for recognition and does not simultaneously apply for enforcement, the period for applying for enforcement shall be recalculated from the date on which the ruling of the People's Court on the recognition application takes effect.

<a id="art-546"></a>第五百四十六条　承认和执行外国法院作出的发生法律效力的判决、裁定或者外国仲裁裁决的案件，人民法院应当组成合议庭进行审查。  
人民法院应当将申请书送达被申请人。被申请人可以陈述意见。  
人民法院经审查作出的裁定，一经送达即发生法律效力。

**Article 546** For a case involving recognition and enforcement of a legally effective judgment or ruling rendered by a foreign court or a foreign arbitral award, the People's Court shall form a collegiate bench for examination.  
The People's Court shall serve the application on the respondent. The respondent may present its opinions.  
A ruling made by the People's Court after examination shall become legally effective upon service.

<a id="art-547"></a>第五百四十七条　与中华人民共和国没有司法协助条约又无互惠关系的国家的法院，未通过外交途径，直接请求人民法院提供司法协助的，人民法院应予退回，并说明理由。

**Article 547** Where a court of a country that has neither a judicial assistance treaty nor a reciprocal relationship with the People's Republic of China directly requests the People's Court to provide judicial assistance without going through diplomatic channels, the People's Court shall return the request and state the reasons.

<a id="art-548"></a>第五百四十八条　当事人在中华人民共和国领域外使用中华人民共和国法院的判决书、裁定书，要求中华人民共和国法院证明其法律效力的，或者外国法院要求中华人民共和国法院证明判决书、裁定书的法律效力的，作出判决、裁定的中华人民共和国法院，可以本法院的名义出具证明。

**Article 548** Where a party uses a judgment or ruling of a court of the People's Republic of China outside the territory of the People's Republic of China and requests a court of the People's Republic of China to certify the legal effect of the judgment or ruling, or where a foreign court requests a court of the People's Republic of China to certify the legal effect of a judgment or ruling, the court of the People's Republic of China that rendered the judgment or ruling may issue a certificate in the name of that court.

<a id="art-549"></a>第五百四十九条　人民法院审理涉及香港、澳门特别行政区和台湾地区的民事诉讼案件，可以参照适用涉外民事诉讼程序的特别规定。

**Article 549** Where the People's Court hears civil cases involving the Hong Kong Special Administrative Region, the Macao Special Administrative Region, and the Taiwan region, it may apply, by reference, the special provisions on procedures for foreign-related civil litigation.

### 二十三、附则

<a id="art-550"></a>第五百五十条　本解释公布施行后，最高人民法院于1992年7月14日发布的《关于适用〈中华人民共和国民事诉讼法〉若干问题的意见》同时废止；最高人民法院以前发布的司法解释与本解释不一致的，不再适用。

**Article 550** After this Interpretation is promulgated and comes into force, the Opinions on Several Issues concerning the Application of the Civil Procedure Law of the People's Republic of China issued by the Supreme People's Court on July 14, 1992 shall be abolished simultaneously; and any judicial interpretation previously issued by the Supreme People's Court that is inconsistent with this Interpretation shall no longer be applied.
